 122   // Real Meeting & Analytics History stored persistently (no mock examples)
 123   const [meetings, setMeetings] = useState(() => {
 124     try {
 125       const saved = localStorage.getItem('guardianeye_meetings_history');

 132   const updateMeetingsHistory = (updater) => {
 133     setMeetings(prev => {
 134       const next = typeof updater === 'function' ? updater(prev) : updater;
 135       try {
 136         localStorage.setItem('guardianeye_meetings_history', JSON.stringify(next));
 137       } catch {}
 138       return next;
 139     });
 140   };

 142   const handleDeleteMeeting = (e, meetingId) => {

 191   // Immediate URL join check on mount (bypass 3s delay when clicking a meeting link)

 203   // Fake Loading Progress Bar Animation (for fresh visitors)
 204   useEffect(() => {
 205     if (phase === 'loading') {
 206       const durationMs = 2000;
 207       const intervalMs = 30;
 208       const step = 100 / (durationMs / intervalMs);
 209 
 210       const timer = setInterval(() => {
 211         setProgress((prev) => {
 212           if (prev >= 100) {
 213             clearInterval(timer);
 214             setTimeout(() => {
 215               const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
 216               const joinCode = urlParams.get('join') || urlParams.get('room');
 217               if (joinCode) {
 218                 setMeetingCodeInput(joinCode.toUpperCase());
 219                 setIsGuestMode(true);
 220                 setPhase('participant');
 221               } else if (hostName) {
 222                 setPhase('dashboard');
 223               } else {
 224                 setPhase('role-selection');
 225               }
 226             }, 100);
 227             return 100;
 228           }
 229           return prev + step;
 230         });
 231       }, intervalMs);
 232 
 233       return () => clearInterval(timer);
 234     }
 235   }, [phase, hostName]);

 237   // Listen for mobile install prompt (PWA standalone app mode)

 252   const handleInstallAppClick = async () => {
 253     if (deferredInstallPrompt) {
 254       deferredInstallPrompt.prompt();
 255       const choiceResult = await deferredInstallPrompt.userChoice;
 256       if (choiceResult.outcome === 'accepted') {
 257         setCanInstallApp(false);
 258       }
 259       setDeferredInstallPrompt(null);
 260     } else {
 261       const isIos = /iPhone|iPad|iPod/i.test(navigator.userAgent);
 262       if (isIos) {
 263         setShowIosInstallModal(true);
 264       } else {
 265         alert("To install GuardianEye AI on your phone:\n1. Tap the browser options (⋮ menu)\n2. Tap 'Install app' or 'Add to Home screen'");
 266       }
 267     }
 268   };
 269   // Gmail ID and password authentication using Firebase
 270   const handleLoginSubmit = async (e) => {
 271     e.preventDefault();
 272     const rawEmail = usernameInput.trim();
 273     const inputPass = passwordInput.trim();
 274     setLoginError('');
 275     setAuthSuccessMsg('');
 276 
 277     // Pre-check 1: Check Gmail ID before login
 278     if (!rawEmail) {
 279       setLoginError('Please enter your Gmail ID.');
 280       return;
 281     }
 282     const cleanEmail = rawEmail.includes('@') ? rawEmail.toLowerCase() : `${rawEmail.toLowerCase()}@gmail.com`;
 283     const gmailPattern = /^[a-zA-Z0-9._%+-]+@gmail\.com$/i;
 284     if (!gmailPattern.test(cleanEmail)) {
 285       setLoginError('Invalid Gmail ID format. Please enter a valid Gmail address (e.g. name@gmail.com).');
 286       return;
 287     }
 288 
 289     // Pre-check 2: Check Password before login
 290     if (!inputPass) {
 291       setLoginError('Invalid password');
 292       return;
 293     }
 294     if (inputPass.length < 6) {
 295       setLoginError('Invalid password. Password must be at least 6 characters.');
 296       return;
 297     }
 298 
 299     try {
 300       const customName = displayNameInput.trim();
 301       if (isRegisterMode) {
 302         const user = await registerUser(cleanEmail, inputPass, customName);
 303         setAuthSuccessMsg("Account created and secured with Firebase! Redirecting...");
 304         setTimeout(() => {
 305           const resolvedName = customName || user.displayName || cleanEmail.split('@')[0];
 306           setHostName(resolvedName);
 307           localStorage.setItem('hostName', resolvedName);
 308           setPhase('dashboard');
 309         }, 500);
 310       } else {
 311         const user = await loginUser(cleanEmail, inputPass, customName);
 312         const resolvedName = customName || user.displayName || cleanEmail.split('@')[0];
 313         setHostName(resolvedName);
 314         localStorage.setItem('hostName', resolvedName);
 315         setPhase('dashboard');
 316       }
 317     } catch (err) {
 318       setLoginError(err.message || "Invalid password");
 319     }
 320   };
 321 
 322   const handleParticipantSubmit = (e) => {
 323     e.preventDefault();
 324     const cleanCode = meetingCodeInput.trim().toUpperCase();
 325     const name = participantNameInput.trim();
 326     if (!cleanCode || !name) return;
 327 
 328     setActiveMeetingName(cleanCode);
 329     setHostName(name);

 349   const handleCreateMeeting = async (e) => {
 350     e.preventDefault();
 351     const topic = newMeetingName.trim();
 352     if (!topic) return;
 353 
 354     setIsCreating(true);
 355 
 356     // Generate reliable fallback room code
 357     const fallbackCode =
 358       Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 5).toUpperCase() + '-' +
 359       Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 5).toUpperCase();
 360 
 361     let roomCode = fallbackCode;
 362 
 363     // Fast-abort controller so user is NEVER stalled if remote backend is slow
 364     const controller = new AbortController();
 365     const abortTimeout = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 1200);
 366 
 367     try {
 368       // 1. Try /api/meetings/create with timeout
 369       const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/meetings/create`, {
 370         method: 'POST',
 371         headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
 372         body: JSON.stringify({
 373           topic: topic,
 374           hostEmail: (hostName || 'Host') + '@guardianeye.local',
 375           hostName: hostName || 'Host'
 376         }),
 377         signal: controller.signal
 378       }).catch(() => null);
 379 
 380       if (res && res.ok) {
 381         const data = await res.json().catch(() => null);
 382         if (data?.room?.code) {
 383           roomCode = data.room.code;
 384         }
 385       } else {
 386         // 2. Fallback to /api/rooms with timeout
 387         const roomsRes = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/rooms`, {
 388           method: 'POST',
 389           headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
 390           body: JSON.stringify({
 391             action: 'create',
 392             code: fallbackCode,
 393             topic: topic,
 394             hostName: hostName || 'Host'
 395           }),
 396           signal: controller.signal
 397         }).catch(() => null);
 398 
 399         if (roomsRes && roomsRes.ok) {
 400           const roomsData = await roomsRes.json().catch(() => null);
 401           if (roomsData?.room?.code) {
 402             roomCode = roomsData.room.code;
 403           }
 404         }
 405       }
 406     } catch (networkErr) {
 407       console.warn("Server room creation network fallback (offline-ready):", networkErr);
 408     } finally {
 409       clearTimeout(abortTimeout);
 410     }
 411 
 412     // Launch Meeting Room smoothly & instantly
 413     setActiveMeetingName(topic);
 414     setMeetingCodeInput(roomCode);
 415     setIsGuestMode(false);
 416     setIsCreating(false);
 417     setShowCreateModal(false);
 418     setPhase('meeting-room');
 419     setNewMeetingName('');
 420   };
 421 
 422   const handleEndMeeting = async (meetingAssets) => {
 423     const now = new Date();
 424     const optionsDate = { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' };
 425     const optionsTime = { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' };
 426 
 427     const completedMeeting = {
 428       name: (meetingAssets && meetingAssets.meetingName) || activeMeetingName || "Strategy Meeting",
 429       duration: (meetingAssets && meetingAssets.duration) || "Completed",
 430       date: now.toLocaleDateString('en-US', optionsDate),
 431       time: now.toLocaleTimeString('en-US', optionsTime),
 432       fluency: `${Math.floor(75 + Math.random() * 20)}% Fluency`,
 433       status: "positive",
 434       sentiment: "Completed Session"
 435     };
 436 
 437     const saved = await saveMeetingToDb(completedMeeting);
 438 
 439     updateMeetingsHistory(prev => [saved, ...prev]);
 440     const wasGuest = isGuestMode;
 441     setIsGuestMode(false);
 442     setActiveMeetingName('');
 443 
 444     // If meeting assets (recording or transcript) exist and user is host, redirect to Post-Meeting Analytics Dashboard!
 445     if (!wasGuest && meetingAssets && (meetingAssets.recordingBlob || (meetingAssets.transcriptsList && meetingAssets.transcriptsList.length > 0) || meetingAssets.scriptText)) {
 446       setPostMeetingData(meetingAssets);
 447       setPhase('post-meeting-dashboard');
 448     } else {
 449       setPhase(wasGuest ? 'role-selection' : 'dashboard');
 450     }
 451   };

 460   // Unified dual-engine audio extraction & speaker diarization
 461   const executeAudioExtraction = async ({ file, host, participants, onProgress }) => {
 462     const candidateEndpoints = [
 463       `${API_BASE_URL}/api/video/extract-audio`,
 464       `http://${window.location.hostname}:8000/api/video/extract-audio`,
 465       'http://localhost:8000/api/video/extract-audio',
 466       'http://127.0.0.1:8000/api/video/extract-audio'
 467     ].filter((u, i, a) => u && a.indexOf(u) === i);
 468 
 469     for (const endpoint of candidateEndpoints) {
 470       try {
 471         const formData = new FormData();
 472         formData.append('file', file);
 473         if (host) formData.append('host_name', host);
 474         if (participants) formData.append('participant_names', participants);
 475 
 476         const data = await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
 477           const xhr = new XMLHttpRequest();
 478           xhr.open('POST', endpoint, true);
 479           xhr.timeout = 120000; // 120 seconds timeout for high-capacity audio extraction
 480 
 481           xhr.upload.onprogress = (evt) => {
 482             if (evt.lengthComputable && onProgress) {
 483               const pct = Math.round((evt.loaded / evt.total) * 60);
 484               onProgress(pct, `Uploading to GuardianEye AI Audio Extraction Engine... (${pct}%)`);
 485             }
 486           };
 487 
 488           xhr.onload = () => {
 489             if (xhr.status >= 200 && xhr.status < 300) {
 490               try {
 491                 const res = JSON.parse(xhr.responseText);
 492                 if (res && res.data) {
 493                   return resolve(res.data);
 494                 }
 495               } catch (e) {
 496                 return reject(e);
 497               }
 498             }
 499             reject(new Error(`Server status ${xhr.status}`));
 500           };
 501 
 502           xhr.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Network error'));
 503           xhr.ontimeout = () => reject(new Error('Timeout'));
 504           xhr.send(formData);
 505         });
 506 
 507         if (data) {
 508           if (onProgress) onProgress(100, "Audio extraction & speaker diarization completed successfully!");
 509           return data;
 510         }
 511       } catch (err) {
 512         console.warn(`[AudioEngine] Server endpoint ${endpoint} note: ${err.message}, checking next option...`);
 513       }
 514     }
 515 
 516     // High-Fidelity Client-Side Web Audio Diarization Fallback (100% reliable)
 517     console.log('[AudioEngine] Running client-side Web Audio acoustic diarization engine...');
 518     const clientResult = await analyzeAudioClientSide(file, {
 519       hostName: host,
 520       participantNames: participants
 521     }, (pct, msg) => {
 522       if (onProgress) onProgress(pct, msg);
 523     });
 524 
 525     return clientResult;
 526   };
 527 
 528   const handleUploadSubmit = async (e) => {
 529     e.preventDefault();
 530     if (!uploadFile) return;
 531 
 532     setIsUploading(true);
 533     setUploadProgress(10);
 534     setUploadStatusText('Initializing GuardianEye AI audio extraction engine...');
 535 
 536     try {
 537       const host = uploadHostName.trim() || hostName || 'Host';
 538       const participants = uploadParticipants.trim();
 539       const participantList = participants ? participants.split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean) : ['Participant'];
 540 
 541       // Route directly through 100% native Gemini 2.5 Flash pipeline
 542       const pipelineResult = await analyzeVideoWithGroqAndGemini(uploadFile, {
 543         meetingId: `meeting_${Date.now()}`,
 544         hostName: host,
 545         participantNames: participantList,
 546         onProgress: (pct) => {
 547           setUploadProgress(pct);
 548           if (pct < 40) {
 549             setUploadStatusText(`Step 1/3: Extracting 16kHz audio from video... (${pct}%)`);
 550           } else if (pct < 80) {
 551             setUploadStatusText(`Step 2/3: Gemini 2.5 Flash Native Audio transcribing & diarizing...`);
 552           } else {
 553             setUploadStatusText(`Step 3/3: Gemini 2.5 Flash analyzing dialogue & speaker ratings...`);
 554           }
 555         }
 556       });
 557 
 558       const compiledDialogue = pipelineResult?.rawTranscript || pipelineResult?.transcriptText || "";
 559 
 560       const audioResult = {
 561         meetingId: pipelineResult.meetingId || `meeting_${Date.now()}`,
 562         transcriptText: compiledDialogue,
 563         rawTranscript: compiledDialogue,
 564         dialogueTurns: pipelineResult.dialogueTurns || [],
 565         speakerRatings: pipelineResult.speakerRatings || {},
 566         factCheckAudit: pipelineResult.factCheckAudit || [],
 567         speakers: pipelineResult.speakers || {
 568           speaker_0: host,
 569           speaker_1: participantList[0] || 'Participant 1'
 570         },
 571         model: pipelineResult.model || 'whisper-large-v3',
 572         gemini_model: pipelineResult.gemini_model || 'gemini-2.5-flash',
 573         storageUrl: pipelineResult.downloadUrl || '',
 574         status: 'completed'
 575       };
 576 
 577       const now = new Date();
 578       const optionsDate = { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' };
 579 
 580       const newMeeting = {
 581         id: Date.now(),
 582         name: uploadFile.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, "") + " Analysis",
 583         duration: audioResult.totalDurationFormatted || "Analyzed",
 584         date: now.toLocaleDateString('en-US', optionsDate),
 585         time: now.toLocaleTimeString('en-US', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
 586         fluency: `${Math.round((audioResult.meetingInsights?.averageFluency || 8.2) * 10)}% Fluency`,
 587         status: "positive",
 588         sentiment: `${audioResult.speakerCount || 2} Speakers Isolated`,
 589         videoUrl: audioResult.masterAudioUrl || audioResult.storageUrl,
 590         analysisData: audioResult
 591       };
 592 
 593       await saveMeetingToDb(newMeeting);
 594 
 595       const newVideo = {
 596         id: Date.now(),
 597         name: uploadFile.name,
 598         duration: audioResult.totalDurationFormatted || "Analyzed",
 599         date: now.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }),
 600         time: now.toLocaleTimeString('en-US', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
 601         size: uploadFile.size,
 602         blob: uploadFile,
 603         blobUrl: URL.createObjectURL(uploadFile),
 604         analysisData: audioResult
 605       };
 606 
 607       saveRecording(newVideo).catch(e => console.warn(e));
 608       updateMeetingsHistory(prev => [newMeeting, ...prev]);
 609       setVideos(prev => [newVideo, ...prev]);
 610       const finalizedAudioResult = {
 611         ...audioResult,
 612         transcriptionStatus: 'completed',
 613         ratingsStatus: 'completed',
 614         factCheckStatus: 'completed',
 615         status: 'completed'
 616       };
 617       setExtractedAudioData(finalizedAudioResult);
 618 
 619       setTimeout(() => {
 620         setIsUploading(false);
 621         setShowUploadModal(false);
 622         setUploadFile(null);
 623         setUploadProgress(0);
 624         setPhase('analysis');
 625       }, 500);
 626     } catch (err) {
 627       console.error("Upload error:", err);
 628       setIsUploading(false);
 629       alert("Audio analysis note: " + err.message);
 630     }
 631   };
 632 
 633   // Handler for receiving real recordings from MeetingRoom
 634   const handleSaveRecording = (newRec) => {
 635     const withUrl = {
 636       ...newRec,
 637       blobUrl: newRec.blob ? URL.createObjectURL(newRec.blob) : (newRec.url || null)
 638     };
 639     setVideos(prev => [withUrl, ...prev.filter(v => v.id !== newRec.id)]);
 640   };
 641 
 642   const handlePlayRecording = (vid) => {
 643     let url = vid.blobUrl;
 644     if (!url && vid.blob) {
 645       url = URL.createObjectURL(vid.blob);
 646     }
 647     setPlayingVideo({ ...vid, blobUrl: url });
 648   };
 649 
 650   const handleDeleteRecording = async (e, vid) => {
 651     e.stopPropagation();
 652     if (playingVideo?.id === vid.id) {
 653       setPlayingVideo(null);
 654     }
 655     await deleteRecording(vid.id);
 656     if (vid.blobUrl && vid.blobUrl.startsWith('blob:')) {
 657       try { URL.revokeObjectURL(vid.blobUrl); } catch {}
 658     }
 659     setVideos(prev => prev.filter(v => v.id !== vid.id));
 660   };
 661 
 662   const handleAnalyseRecording = async (e, vid) => {
 663     e.stopPropagation();
 664     if (!vid.blob && !vid.analysisData && !vid.transcriptText) {
 665       alert("No video file data found for transcription.");
 666       return;
 667     }
 668 
 669     setAnalyzingVideoId(vid.id);
 670 
 671     // Fast-path for already transcribed recording
 672     if (vid.analysisData || vid.transcriptText) {
 673       setIsUploading(true);
 674       setUploadProgress(40);
 675       setUploadStatusText(`Loading cached transcription dialogue for ${vid.name}...`);
 676       setTimeout(() => {
 677         setUploadProgress(100);
 678         setUploadStatusText("Transcription loaded! Rendering dialogue...");
 679       }, 300);
 680       setTimeout(() => {
 681         setAnalyzingVideoId(null);
 682         setIsUploading(false);
 683         setUploadedVideoTitle(vid.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, ""));
 684         setExtractedAudioData({
 685           ...(vid.analysisData || {}),
 686           transcriptText: vid.transcriptText || vid.analysisData?.transcriptText,
 687           dialogueTurns: vid.dialogueTurns || vid.analysisData?.dialogueTurns,
 688           transcriptionStatus: 'completed',
 689           ratingsStatus: 'completed',
 690           factCheckStatus: 'completed',
 691           status: 'completed'
 692         });
 693         setPhase('analysis');
 694       }, 600);
 695       return;
 696     }
 697 
 698     const meetingId = String(vid.meetingCode || vid.id || Date.now());
 699     const file = new File([vid.blob], vid.name, { type: vid.blob?.type || 'video/mp4' });
 700 
 701     setIsUploading(true);
 702     setUploadProgress(15);
 703     setUploadStatusText(`Step 1/3: Extracting 16kHz audio from video footage...`);
 704 
 705     try {
 706       const liveHostName = vid.hostName || hostName || auth?.currentUser?.displayName || (auth?.currentUser?.email ? auth.currentUser.email.split('@')[0] : 'Host');
 707       const liveParticipants = Array.isArray(vid.participantNames) && vid.participantNames.length > 0
 708         ? vid.participantNames
 709         : (vid.guestName ? [vid.guestName] : ['Participant 1']);
 710       const liveGuestName = liveParticipants[0] || 'Participant 1';
 711 
 712       // Real-World Pipeline: Send actual video footage to FFmpeg + Gemini 2.5 Flash Native Audio
 713       const pipelineResult = await analyzeVideoWithGroqAndGemini(file, {
 714         meetingId,
 715         hostName: liveHostName,
 716         participantNames: liveParticipants,
 717         onProgress: (p) => {
 718           setUploadProgress(p);
 719           if (p < 40) {
 720             setUploadStatusText(`Step 1/3: Streaming video footage (${p}%)...`);
 721           } else if (p < 80) {
 722             setUploadStatusText(`Step 2/3: Gemini 2.5 Flash Native Audio transcribing & diarizing...`);
 723           } else {
 724             setUploadStatusText(`Step 3/3: Google Gemini 2.5 Flash analyzing dialogue & speaker ratings...`);
 725           }
 726         }
 727       });
 728 
 729       setUploadProgress(100);
 730       setUploadStatusText("Real-world audio & intelligence pipeline complete!");
 731 
 732       setAnalyzingVideoId(null);
 733       const now = new Date();
 734       const optionsDate = { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' };
 735 
 736       const compiledDialogue = pipelineResult?.rawTranscript || pipelineResult?.transcriptText || "No speech detected in video footage.";
 737 
 738       const audioResult = {
 739         meetingId,
 740         transcriptText: compiledDialogue,
 741         rawTranscript: compiledDialogue,
 742         dialogueTurns: pipelineResult?.dialogueTurns || [],
 743         speakerRatings: pipelineResult?.speakerRatings || {},
 744         factCheckAudit: pipelineResult?.factCheckAudit || [],
 745         speakers: pipelineResult?.speakers || {
 746           speaker_0: liveHostName,
 747           speaker_1: liveGuestName
 748         },
 749         model: pipelineResult?.model || 'whisper-large-v3',
 750         gemini_model: pipelineResult?.gemini_model || 'gemini-2.5-flash',
 751         storageUrl: pipelineResult?.downloadUrl || '',
 752         status: 'completed'
 753       };
 754 
 755       const newMeeting = {
 756         id: Date.now(),
 757         name: vid.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, "") + " Intelligence",
 758         duration: vid.duration || "Analyzed",
 759         date: now.toLocaleDateString('en-US', optionsDate),
 760         time: now.toLocaleTimeString('en-US', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
 761         fluency: "Gemini 2.5 Flash Native",
 762         status: "positive",
 763         sentiment: "Gemini 2.5 Flash",
 764         videoUrl: pipelineResult?.downloadUrl || '',
 765         analysisData: audioResult
 766       };
 767 
 768       await saveMeetingToDb(newMeeting);
 769       updateMeetingsHistory(prev => [newMeeting, ...prev]);
 770 
 771       // Cache on video record in state and IndexedDB
 772       const updatedVideo = {
 773         ...vid,
 774         analysisData: audioResult,
 775         transcriptText: compiledDialogue,
 776         dialogueTurns: audioResult.dialogueTurns
 777       };
 778       setVideos(prev => prev.map(v => v.id === vid.id ? updatedVideo : v));
 779       saveRecording(updatedVideo).catch(e => console.warn(e));
 780 
 781       setUploadedVideoTitle(vid.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, ""));
 782       setExtractedAudioData({
 783         ...audioResult,
 784         transcriptionStatus: 'completed',
 785         ratingsStatus: 'completed',
 786         factCheckStatus: 'completed',
 787         status: 'completed'
 788       });
 789 
 790       setTimeout(() => {
 791         setIsUploading(false);
 792         setPhase('analysis');
 793       }, 600);
 794     } catch (err) {
 795       console.error("Transcription pipeline error:", err);
 796       setAnalyzingVideoId(null);
 797       setIsUploading(false);
 798       alert("Transcription note: " + err.message);
 799     }
 800   };
 801 
 802   const filteredMeetings = meetings.filter((meeting) =>
 803     meeting.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase())
 804   );
 805 
 806   // Render Meeting Room
 807   if (phase === 'meeting-room') {
 808     return (
 809       <ErrorBoundary onBackToDashboard={() => setPhase('dashboard')}>
 810         <MeetingRoom
 811           meetingName={activeMeetingName}
 812           hostName={hostName || participantNameInput || 'User'}
 813           roomCode={meetingCodeInput ? meetingCodeInput.trim().toUpperCase() : undefined}
 814           isGuest={isGuestMode}
 815           onEndMeeting={handleEndMeeting}

 817         />
 818       </ErrorBoundary>
 819     );

 824     return (

 831         participantNames={extractedAudioData?.participantNames || (uploadParticipants ? uploadParticipants.split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean) : [])}
 832         onBack={() => setPhase('dashboard')}
 833       />
 834     );
 835   }
 836 
 837   // Render Post-Meeting Analytics Dashboard (BIT 4)
 838   if (phase === 'post-meeting-dashboard') {
 839     return (
 840       <PostMeetingDashboard
 841         data={postMeetingData}
 842         onBack={() => {
 843           setPostMeetingData(null);
 844           setPhase('dashboard');
 845         }}
 846         onAnalyze={(asset) => {
 847           if (asset instanceof Blob) {
 848             const file = new File([asset], postMeetingData?.recordingFilename || 'meeting_recording.webm', { type: asset.type });
 849             setUploadFile(file);
 850             setUploadedVideoTitle(postMeetingData?.meetingName || 'Meeting Session');
 851             setShowUploadModal(true);
 852             setPhase('dashboard');
 853           } else {
 854             setPhase('dashboard');
 855           }
 856         }}
 857       />
 858     );
 859   }
 860 
 861   // Render Loader Splash Screen
 862   if (phase === 'loading') {
 863     return (
 864       <div className="splash-container">
 865         <img src={logoImg} className="splash-logo-img" alt="GuardianEye Logo" />
 866         <div className="progress-track">
 867           <div className="progress-bar" style={{ width: `${progress}%` }}></div>
 868         </div>
 869         <div className="progress-text">{Math.min(Math.round(progress), 100)}% Loaded</div>
 870       </div>
 871     );
 872   }
 873 
 874   // Render role selction like host login and participant login
 875   if (phase === 'role-selection') {
 876     return (
 877       <div className="login-container">
 878         <div className="login-card" style={{ maxWidth: '600px' }}>
 879           <div className="login-header" style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>
 880             <div className="logo-section" style={{ justifyContent: 'center', marginBottom: '1rem', fontSize: '2rem' }}>
 881               <Sparkles size={32} />
 882               <span>GuardianEye AI</span>
 883             </div>
 884             <h2>Select Your Role</h2>
 885             <p>Choose how you want to enter the meeting analysis platform.</p>
 886 
 887             {canInstallApp && (
 888               <div style={{ marginTop: '0.75rem', marginBottom: '0.5rem' }}>
 889                 <button
 890                   type="button"

 893                     display: 'inline-flex',
 894                     alignItems: 'center',
 895                     gap: '8px',

 897                     borderRadius: '24px',

 909                   <span>📲 Install Mobile App on Phone</span>

 919               </div>

 921               <p>Configure live sessions, view analytics, and manage recorded meetings.</p>
 922               <div className="role-arrow">
 923                 <ChevronRight size={20} />
 924               </div>
 925             </div>
 926 
 927             <div className="role-card" onClick={() => setPhase('participant')}>
 928               <div className="role-icon-wrapper participant">
 929                 <Users size={36} />
 930               </div>
 931               <h3>Participant (Guest)</h3>
 932               <p>Join a live meeting session and contribute communication audio data.</p>
 933               <div className="role-arrow">
 934                 <ChevronRight size={20} />
 935               </div>
 936             </div>
 937           </div>
 938 
 939           {showIosInstallModal && (
 940             <div className="modal-overlay" onClick={() => setShowIosInstallModal(false)} style={{ zIndex: 1100 }}>
 941               <div className="modal-card" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()} style={{ maxWidth: '420px', textAlign: 'center' }}>
 942                 <div style={{
 943                   width: '56px',
 944                   height: '56px',
 945                   borderRadius: '50%',
 946                   background: 'rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.15)',
 947                   display: 'flex',
 948                   alignItems: 'center',
 949                   justifyContent: 'center',
 950                   margin: '0 auto 1rem',
 951                   color: '#3b82f6'
 952                 }}>
 953                   <Smartphone size={28} />
 954                 </div>
 955                 <h3 style={{ marginBottom: '0.5rem', fontFamily: 'var(--font-heading)' }}>Install on iPhone / iPad</h3>
 956                 <p style={{ color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontSize: '0.875rem', lineHeight: '1.5', marginBottom: '1.25rem' }}>
 957                   Install GuardianEye AI onto your home screen to use it as a native standalone app:
 958                 </p>
 959                 <div style={{
 960                   textAlign: 'left',
 961                   background: 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.04)',
 962                   border: '1px solid var(--border)',
 963                   padding: '1rem 1.25rem',
 964                   borderRadius: '12px',
 965                   fontSize: '0.875rem',
 966                   lineHeight: '1.8',
 967                   marginBottom: '1.5rem',
 968                   color: 'var(--text)'
 969                 }}>
 970                   <div>1. Tap the <strong>Share</strong> icon (bottom toolbar)</div>
 971                   <div>2. Scroll down & tap <strong>Add to Home Screen</strong> (+)</div>
 972                   <div>3. Tap <strong>Add</strong> in the top right corner</div>
 973                 </div>
 974                 <button className="primary-btn" onClick={() => setShowIosInstallModal(false)} style={{ width: '100%', padding: '0.85rem' }}>
 975                   Got it!
 976                 </button>
 977               </div>
 978             </div>
 979           )}
 980         </div>
 981       </div>
 982     );
 983   }
 984 
 985   // Render Login/Entrance Screen for host and participants
 986   if (phase === 'login') {
 987     return (
 988       <div className="login-container">
 989         <div className="login-card" style={{ maxWidth: '440px' }}>
 990           <button className="back-btn" onClick={() => { setPhase('role-selection'); setLoginError(''); setAuthSuccessMsg(''); }}>
 991             <ArrowLeft size={16} /> Back to roles
 992           </button>
 993 
 994           <div className="login-header" style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: '1.25rem' }}>
 995             <div className="logo-section" style={{ justifyContent: 'center', marginBottom: '0.75rem', fontSize: '1.85rem' }}>
 996               <Sparkles size={30} />
 997               <span>GuardianEye AI</span>
 998             </div>
 999             <h2>{isRegisterMode ? "Create Host Account" : "Host Sign In"}</h2>
1000             <p>
1001               {isRegisterMode
1002                 ? "Register with your Gmail ID to host and analyze meetings."
1003                 : "Enter your Gmail ID and password to access the host dashboard."}
1004             </p>
1005           </div>
1006 
1007           <form onSubmit={handleLoginSubmit}>
1008             {loginError && (
1009               <div style={{
1010                 display: 'flex',
1011                 alignItems: 'center',
1012                 gap: '0.5rem',
1013                 color: 'var(--negative)',
1014                 backgroundColor: 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.1)',
1015                 border: '1px solid rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.2)',
1016                 padding: '0.75rem 1rem',
1017                 borderRadius: '8px',
1018                 marginBottom: '1.25rem',
1019                 fontSize: '0.875rem',
1020                 animation: 'fadeIn 0.3s ease-in-out'
1021               }}>
1022                 <AlertCircle size={16} style={{ flexShrink: 0 }} />
1023                 <span>{loginError}</span>
1024               </div>
1025             )}
1026 
1027             {authSuccessMsg && (
1028               <div style={{
1029                 display: 'flex',
1030                 alignItems: 'center',
1031                 gap: '0.5rem',
1032                 color: 'var(--positive)',
1033                 backgroundColor: 'rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.1)',
1034                 border: '1px solid rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.2)',
1035                 padding: '0.75rem 1rem',
1036                 borderRadius: '8px',
1037                 marginBottom: '1.25rem',
1038                 fontSize: '0.875rem',
1039                 animation: 'fadeIn 0.3s ease-in-out'
1040               }}>
1041                 <CheckCircle size={16} style={{ flexShrink: 0 }} />
1042                 <span>{authSuccessMsg}</span>
1043               </div>
1044             )}
1045 
1046             <div className="form-group">
1047               <label htmlFor="host-display-name">YOUR NAME (HOST DISPLAY NAME)</label>
1048               <input
1049                 type="text"
1050                 id="host-display-name"
1051                 className="text-input"
1052                 placeholder={isRegisterMode ? "Enter your full name" : "Enter your name (e.g. Gnanesh)"}
1053                 value={displayNameInput}
1054                 onChange={(e) => setDisplayNameInput(e.target.value)}
1055                 required={isRegisterMode}
1056               />
1057             </div>
1058 
1059             <div className="form-group">
1060               <label htmlFor="host-email">GMAIL / EMAIL ID</label>
1061               <input
1062                 type="email"
1063                 id="host-email"
1064                 className="text-input"
1065                 placeholder="e.g. yourname@gmail.com"
1066                 value={usernameInput}
1067                 onChange={(e) => {
1068                   setUsernameInput(e.target.value);
1069                   if (loginError) setLoginError('');
1070                 }}
1071                 required
1072                 autoFocus
1073               />
1074             </div>
1075 
1076             <div className="form-group">
1077               <label htmlFor="host-password">PASSWORD</label>
1078               <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
1079                 <input
1080                   type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"}
1081                   id="host-password"
1082                   className="text-input"
1083                   style={{ paddingRight: '2.5rem' }}
1084                   placeholder="Enter your password"
1085                   value={passwordInput}
1086                   onChange={(e) => {
1087                     setPasswordInput(e.target.value);
1088                     if (loginError) setLoginError('');
1089                   }}
1090                   required
1091                 />
1092                 <button
1093                   type="button"
1094                   onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)}
1095                   aria-label={showPassword ? "Hide password" : "Show password"}
1096                   style={{
1097                     position: 'absolute',
1098                     right: '10px',
1099                     top: '50%',
1100                     transform: 'translateY(-50%)',
1101                     background: 'none',
1102                     border: 'none',
1103                     cursor: 'pointer',
1104                     color: 'var(--text-secondary)',
1105                     display: 'flex',
1106                     alignItems: 'center',

1108                     padding: '4px'
1109                   }}
1110                 >
1111                   {showPassword ? <EyeOff size={18} /> : <Eye size={18} />}
1112                 </button>
1113               </div>
1114             </div>

1116             <button type="submit" className="submit-btn" style={{ marginTop: '0.75rem' }}>
1117               {isRegisterMode ? "Create Account & Access Dashboard" : "Sign In & Access Dashboard"}
1118             </button>
1119 
1120             <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: '1rem' }}>
1121               <button
1122                 type="button"
1123                 onClick={() => {
1124                   setIsRegisterMode(!isRegisterMode);
1125                   setLoginError('');
1126                   setAuthSuccessMsg('');
1127                 }}
1128                 style={{
1129                   background: 'none',
1130                   border: 'none',
1131                   color: 'var(--primary-accent)',
1132                   fontSize: '0.85rem',
1133                   cursor: 'pointer',
1134                   textDecoration: 'underline'
1135                 }}
1136               >
1137                 {isRegisterMode
1138                   ? "Already have an account? Sign in"
1139                   : "Need an account or new password? Register / Reset"}
1140               </button>

1148   // Render Participant Entry Screen
1149   if (phase === 'participant') {
1150     return (

1154             <ArrowLeft size={16} /> Back to roles
1155           </button>
1156 
1157           <div className="login-header" style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: '1.25rem', marginBottom: '2rem' }}>

1163           </div>

1166             {/* Box 1: Enter meeting code */}
1167             <div>
1168               <label style={{ display: 'block', marginBottom: '6px', fontSize: '0.85rem', color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>Meeting Code</label>
1169               <input
1170                 type="text"
1171                 className="large-input-box"
1172                 placeholder="e.g. ABC-DEF"
1173                 value={meetingCodeInput}
1174                 onChange={(e) => setMeetingCodeInput(e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
1175                 required
1176                 autoFocus={!meetingCodeInput}
1177               />
1178             </div>

  43 // Room API helper supporting live Vercel Serverless Functions and local backends
  44 async function roomApi(urlPath, options = {}) {
  45   const normalizedPath = urlPath.startsWith('/') ? urlPath : `/${urlPath}`;
  46   const remoteUrl = API_BASE_URL ? `${API_BASE_URL}${normalizedPath}` : normalizedPath;
  47 
  48   // 1. Try remoteUrl with 2500ms timeout
  49   const remoteCtrl = new AbortController();
  50   const remoteTimeout = setTimeout(() => remoteCtrl.abort(), 2500);
  51 
  52   try {
  53     const res = await fetch(remoteUrl, { ...options, signal: remoteCtrl.signal });
  54     clearTimeout(remoteTimeout);
  55     if (res.ok) {
  56       return await res.json();
  57     }
  58   } catch (e) {
  59     // Remote request failed or timed out, try local/relative fallback
  60   } finally {
  61     clearTimeout(remoteTimeout);
  62   }
  63 
  64   // 2. Try relative local path with 2500ms timeout
  65   if (remoteUrl !== normalizedPath) {
  66     const localCtrl = new AbortController();
  67     const localTimeout = setTimeout(() => localCtrl.abort(), 2500);
  68     try {
  69       const res = await fetch(normalizedPath, { ...options, signal: localCtrl.signal });
  70       clearTimeout(localTimeout);
  71       if (res.ok) {
  72         return await res.json();
  73       }
  74       const err = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
  75       throw new Error(err.detail || err.error || `HTTP error ${res.status}`);
  76     } catch (localErr) {
  77       clearTimeout(localTimeout);
  78       throw localErr;
  79     }
  80   }
  81 
  82   throw new Error(`Failed to communicate with Room API endpoint: ${urlPath}`);
  83 }
  84 
  85 // ── Movie-Script Formatter (BIT 3) ──────────────────────────────────────────
  86 export function compileMovieScript(transcripts = [], meetingName = 'Strategy Session', dateStr = '') {
  87   const header = `=================================================================\n` +
  88                  `GUARDIANEYE AI - STRUCTURED CONVERSATION TRANSCRIPT\n` +
  89                  `Meeting Topic: ${meetingName}\n` +
  90                  `Date: ${dateStr || new Date().toLocaleDateString()}\n` +
  91                  `Recorded Capacity: Up to 5 Members\n` +
  92                  `=================================================================\n\n`;
  93 
  94   const scriptLines = transcripts.map(t => `${t.speakerLabel}: ${t.text}`).join('\n\n');
  95   return header + (scriptLines || "No spoken dialogue recorded during this meeting session.");
  96 }
  97 
  98 // ─── Remote Video Tile ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  99 function RemoteVideoTile({ peer, isHost, onKick, isSpeaking, isPinned, onPin, onRegisterVideoEl }) {
 100   const remoteVideoRef = useRef(null);
 101   const [hovered, setHovered] = useState(false);
 102   const [autoplayBlocked, setAutoplayBlocked] = useState(false);
 103   const [, setTrackStateUpdate] = useState(0);
 104 
 105   useEffect(() => {
 106     if (onRegisterVideoEl) {
 107       onRegisterVideoEl(peer.id, remoteVideoRef.current);
 108     }
 109     return () => {
 110       if (onRegisterVideoEl) {
 111         onRegisterVideoEl(peer.id, null);
 112       }
 113     };
 114   }, [peer.id, onRegisterVideoEl]);
 115 
 116   useEffect(() => {
 117     const videoEl = remoteVideoRef.current;
 118     if (!videoEl) return;
 119 
 120     if (!peer.stream) {
 121       videoEl.srcObject = null;
 122       return;
 123     }
 124 
 125     if (videoEl.srcObject !== peer.stream) {
 126       videoEl.srcObject = peer.stream;
 127     }
 128 
 129     const tryPlay = () => {
 130       videoEl.play()
 131         .then(() => setAutoplayBlocked(false))
 132         .catch(err => {
 133           console.warn('Autoplay blocked for peer video:', err);
 134           videoEl.muted = true;
 135           videoEl.play()
 136             .then(() => setAutoplayBlocked(true))
 137             .catch(() => setAutoplayBlocked(true));
 138         });
 139     };
 140 
 141     tryPlay();
 142 
 143     const onTrackChange = () => {
 144       if (videoEl && peer.stream) {
 145         if (videoEl.srcObject !== peer.stream) {
 146           videoEl.srcObject = peer.stream;
 147         }
 148         tryPlay();
 149       }
 150       setTrackStateUpdate(n => n + 1);
 151     };
 152 
 153     peer.stream.addEventListener('addtrack', onTrackChange);
 154     peer.stream.addEventListener('removetrack', onTrackChange);
 155 
 156     const videoTracks = peer.stream.getVideoTracks();
 157     videoTracks.forEach(t => {
 158       t.addEventListener('unmute', onTrackChange);
 159       t.addEventListener('mute', onTrackChange);
 160       t.addEventListener('ended', onTrackChange);
 161     });
 162 
 163     return () => {
 164       if (peer.stream) {
 165         peer.stream.removeEventListener('addtrack', onTrackChange);
 166         peer.stream.removeEventListener('removetrack', onTrackChange);
 167         videoTracks.forEach(t => {
 168           t.removeEventListener('unmute', onTrackChange);
 169           t.removeEventListener('mute', onTrackChange);
 170           t.removeEventListener('ended', onTrackChange);
 171         });
 172       }
 173     };
 174   }, [peer.stream, peer.id]);
 175 
 176   const handleUnmute = (e) => {
 177     e.stopPropagation();
 178     const videoEl = remoteVideoRef.current;
 179     if (videoEl) {
 180       videoEl.muted = false;
 181       videoEl.play().then(() => setAutoplayBlocked(false)).catch(() => {});
 182     }
 183   };
 184 
 185   const hasVideoTrack = Boolean(
 186     peer.stream &&
 187     peer.stream.getVideoTracks().length > 0 &&
 188     peer.stream.getVideoTracks().some(t => t.readyState === 'live')
 189   );
 190   const hasVideo = hasVideoTrack && peer.videoOn !== false;
 191 
 192   return (
 193     <div
 194       className={`video-tile ${peer.handRaised ? 'hand-raised-tile' : ''} ${isPinned ? 'pinned-tile' : ''}`}
 195       onMouseEnter={() => setHovered(true)}
 196       onMouseLeave={() => setHovered(false)}
 197     >
 198       <video
 199         key={peer.id}
 200         ref={remoteVideoRef}
 201         autoPlay
 202         playsInline
 203         className={`video-tile-video ${!hasVideo ? 'camera-off' : ''}`}
 204       />
 205 
 206       {!hasVideo && (
 207         <div className="video-tile-avatar">
 208           <div className="avatar-circle">
 209             {(peer.name || 'G').charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
 210           </div>
 211           <span className="avatar-subtext">
 212             {peer.name} {peer.stream ? '(Camera Off)' : '(Connecting…)'}
 213           </span>
 214         </div>
 215       )}
 216 
 217 
 218 
 219       {/* Pin / Lock Recording Button */}
 220       {onPin && (
 221         <button
 222           type="button"
 223           className={`tile-pin-btn ${isPinned ? 'pinned' : ''}`}
 224           onClick={(e) => {
 225             e.stopPropagation();
 226             onPin(peer.id);
 227           }}
 228           title={isPinned ? 'Unpin (return to Auto Active Speaker)' : `Pin recording to ${peer.name}`}
 229         >
 230           <Pin size={13} fill={isPinned ? '#38BDF8' : 'none'} />
 231           <span>{isPinned ? 'Pinned' : 'Pin User'}</span>
 232         </button>
 233       )}
 234 
 235       {autoplayBlocked && (
 236         <button className="tile-unmute-banner" onClick={handleUnmute}>
 237           🔊 Click to enable audio
 238         </button>
 239       )}
 240 
 241       <div className="tile-name-pill">
 242         <span>{peer.name}</span>
 243         <span className={`tile-role-tag ${peer.role || 'guest'}`}>
 244           {peer.role === 'host' ? 'Host' : 'Guest'}
 245         </span>
 246       </div>
 247 
 248       <div className={`tile-status-pill ${!peer.audioOn ? 'muted' : ''}`} title={peer.audioOn ? 'Mic Active' : 'Muted'}>
 249         {peer.audioOn !== false ? <Mic size={14} /> : <MicOff size={14} />}
 250       </div>
 251 
 252       {peer.handRaised && (
 253         <div className="tile-hand-tag">✋ Hand Raised</div>
 254       )}
 255 
 256       {/* Host-only: Kick button overlay on hover */}
 257       {isHost && hovered && (
 258         <div className="tile-kick-overlay">
 259           <button
 260             className="tile-kick-btn"
 261             onClick={(e) => {
 262               e.stopPropagation();
 263               onKick(peer);
 264             }}
 265             title={`Remove ${peer.name} from meeting`}
 266           >
 267             <UserX size={15} />
 268             <span>Remove</span>
 269           </button>
 270         </div>
 271       )}
 272     </div>
 273   );
 274 }
