 276 // ─── MeetingRoom Component ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
 277 function MeetingRoom({
 278   meetingName,
 279   hostName,
 280   onEndMeeting,
 281   roomCode: propRoomCode,
 282   isGuest = false,
 283   onSaveRecording
 284 }) {
 285   const localVideoRef        = useRef(null);
 286   const streamRef            = useRef(null);     // local MediaStream
 287   const peerInstanceRef      = useRef(null);     // PeerJS Peer instance
 288   const callsRef             = useRef({});       // peerId -> MediaConnection
 289   const dataConnsRef         = useRef({});       // peerId -> DataConnection
 290   const mediaRecorderRef     = useRef(null);
 291   const recordedChunksRef    = useRef([]);
 292   const recordingStartTimeRef= useRef(null);     // Track actual elapsed recording time
 293   const recordingAudioCtxRef = useRef(null);     // Web Audio context for recording mix
 294   const recordingSourcesRef  = useRef([]);        // Retain audio node references to prevent GC
 295   const recognitionRef       = useRef(null);
 296   const isMountedRef         = useRef(true);
 297 
 298   // BIT 1: Screen Recording with getDisplayMedia & Mixed Audio
 299   const displayStreamRef       = useRef(null);
 300   const lastRecordingBlobRef   = useRef(null);
 301   const lastRecordingUrlRef    = useRef(null);
 302   const lastRecordingFilenameRef = useRef(null);
 303   const recordingDestRef       = useRef(null);
 304 
 305   // BIT 2 & 3: WebRTC Data Channel Speech Identity Mapping & Central Script Aggregator
 306   const transcriptLogRef       = useRef([]);
 307   const participantOrderRef    = useRef(new Map());
 308   const [transcriptEntries, setTranscriptEntries] = useState([]);
 309 
 310   // Media state
 311   const [localStream,  setLocalStream]  = useState(null);
 312   const [mediaStatus,  setMediaStatus]  = useState('loading'); // 'loading' | 'ready' | 'error'
 313   const [mediaError,   setMediaError]   = useState('');
 314   const [videoOn,      setVideoOn]      = useState(true);
 315   const [audioOn,      setAudioOn]      = useState(false);
 316   const [handRaised,   setHandRaised]   = useState(false);
 317   const [isRecording,  setIsRecording]  = useState(false);
 318   const [showNotes,    setShowNotes]    = useState(false);
 319   const [peers,        setPeers]        = useState([]);
 320   const [joinRequests, setJoinRequests] = useState([]);
 321   const [liveCaption,  setLiveCaption]  = useState(null);
 322   const [peerStatus,   setPeerStatus]   = useState('connecting');
 323   const [codeCopied,   setCodeCopied]   = useState(false);
 324   const [isWaitingForApproval, setIsWaitingForApproval] = useState(isGuest);
 325   const [kickTarget, setKickTarget] = useState(null);
 326   // Apple Silicon M2 GPU Diarization & Whisper State
 327   const [m2ProcessingStatus, setM2ProcessingStatus] = useState(null);
 328   const [m2ScriptResult, setM2ScriptResult] = useState(null);
 329   const [showScriptModal, setShowScriptModal] = useState(false);
 330   const [scriptCopied, setScriptCopied] = useState(false);
 331   // Firebase Storage Recording Upload & Report Generation State
 332   const [showGenerateReportBtn, setShowGenerateReportBtn] = useState(false);
 333   const [recordedVideoBlob, setRecordedVideoBlob] = useState(null);
 334   const [isUploadingToFirebase, setIsUploadingToFirebase] = useState(false);
 335   const [firebaseUploadProgress, setFirebaseUploadProgress] = useState(0);
 336   const [firebaseUploadStatus, setFirebaseUploadStatus] = useState(null);
 337 
 338   const remoteStreamsRef     = useRef({});       // peerId -> MediaStream (for recording & analysers)
 339   const remoteVideoElsRef    = useRef({});       // peerId -> HTMLVideoElement
 340   const activeSpeakerIdRef   = useRef('local');  // 'local' | peerId
 341   const pinnedSpeakerIdRef   = useRef(null);     // null = auto active speaker, or 'local' | peerId
 342   const activeSpeakerNameRef = useRef(hostName || 'You');
 343   const recordingCanvasRef   = useRef(null);
 344   const recordingAnimFrameRef= useRef(null);
 345   const recordingStreamRef   = useRef(null);
 346   const peersRef             = useRef([]);
 347   const videoOnRef           = useRef(true);
 348   const audioOnRef           = useRef(false);
 349   const setupCallRef         = useRef(null);
 350 
 351   const [activeSpeakerId,   setActiveSpeakerId]   = useState('local');
 352   const [pinnedSpeakerId,   setPinnedSpeakerId]   = useState(null);
 353   const [activeSpeakerName, setActiveSpeakerName] = useState(hostName || 'You');
 354 
 355   useEffect(() => {
 356     peersRef.current = peers;
 357   }, [peers]);
 358 
 359   useEffect(() => {
 360     videoOnRef.current = videoOn;
 361   }, [videoOn]);
 362 
 363   useEffect(() => {
 364     audioOnRef.current = audioOn;
 365   }, [audioOn]);
 366 
 367   const handleRegisterVideoEl = useCallback((peerId, el) => {
 368     if (el) {
 369       remoteVideoElsRef.current[peerId] = el;
 370     } else {
 371       delete remoteVideoElsRef.current[peerId];
 372     }
 373   }, []);
 374 
 375   const handlePinUser = useCallback((targetId) => {
 376     setPinnedSpeakerId(prev => {
 377       const next = prev === targetId ? null : targetId;
 378       pinnedSpeakerIdRef.current = next;
 379       if (next) {
 380         let name = hostName || 'You';
 381         if (next !== 'local') {
 382           const p = peersRef.current.find(x => x.id === next);
 383           if (p) name = p.name;
 384         }
 385         setActiveSpeakerName(name);
 386         activeSpeakerNameRef.current = name;
 387       } else {
 388         let name = hostName || 'You';
 389         if (activeSpeakerIdRef.current !== 'local') {
 390           const p = peersRef.current.find(x => x.id === activeSpeakerIdRef.current);
 391           if (p) name = p.name;
 392         }
 393         setActiveSpeakerName(name);
 394         activeSpeakerNameRef.current = name;
 395       }
 396       return next;
 397     });
 398   }, [hostName]);
 399 
 400   const [meetingCode] = useState(() => {
 401     if (propRoomCode) return propRoomCode.toUpperCase();
 402     const chars = 'ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789';
 403     let code = '';
 404     for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) code += chars[Math.floor(Math.random() * chars.length)];
 405     code += '-';
 406     for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) code += chars[Math.floor(Math.random() * chars.length)];
 407     return code;
 408   });
 409 
 410   const [notesText] = useState(() => {
 411     try { return localStorage.getItem(`guardianeye_notes_${meetingCode}`) || ''; } catch { return ''; }
 412   });
 413   const [notesValue, setNotesValue] = useState(notesText);
 414 
 415   // ── Helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 416   const upsertPeer = useCallback((id, patch) => {
 417     setPeers(prev => {
 418       const idx = prev.findIndex(p => p.id === id);
 419       if (idx === -1) {
 420         return [...prev, { id, name: 'Peer', role: 'participant', stream: null, handRaised: false, audioOn: false, videoOn: true, ...patch }];
 421       }
 422       const updated = [...prev];
 423       updated[idx] = { ...updated[idx], ...patch };
 424       return updated;
 425     });
 426   }, []);
 427 
 428   const removePeer = useCallback((id) => {
 429     if (callsRef.current[id]) {
 430       try { callsRef.current[id].close(); } catch {}
 431       delete callsRef.current[id];
 432     }
 433     if (dataConnsRef.current[id]) {
 434       try { dataConnsRef.current[id].close(); } catch {}
 435       delete dataConnsRef.current[id];
 436     }
 437     delete remoteStreamsRef.current[id];
 438     delete remoteVideoElsRef.current[id];
 439     setPeers(prev => prev.filter(p => p.id !== id));
 440   }, []);
 441 
 442   // Broadcast data payload to all connected data channels
 443   const broadcastData = useCallback((payload) => {
 444     Object.values(dataConnsRef.current).forEach(conn => {
 445       if (conn && conn.open) {
 446         try {
 447           conn.send(payload);
 448         } catch (e) {
 449           console.warn('[PeerJS] Broadcast failed to peer:', e);
 450         }
 451       }
 452     });
 453   }, []);
 454 
 455   // ── 1. Start local camera & mic ───────────────────────────────────────────
 456   const startMedia = useCallback(async () => {
 457     setMediaStatus('loading');
 458     setMediaError('');
 459 
 460     if (typeof navigator === 'undefined' || !navigator.mediaDevices || !navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia) {
 461       const msg = 'Camera and microphone access requires HTTPS or localhost, or your browser does not support media devices.';
 462       console.warn('[MeetingRoom]', msg);
 463       if (!isMountedRef.current) return null;
 464       setMediaStatus('error');
 465       setMediaError(msg);
 466       setVideoOn(false);
 467       return null;
 468     }
 469 
 470     let stream = null;
 471     let videoAcquired = false;
 472     let audioAcquired = false;
 473 
 474     // 1. Try high quality standard user-facing constraints
 475     try {
 476       stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
 477         video: {
 478           width: { ideal: 1280, max: 1920 },
 479           height: { ideal: 720, max: 1080 },
 480           frameRate: { ideal: 30, max: 30 },
 481           facingMode: 'user'
 482         },
 483         audio: {
 484           echoCancellation: true,
 485           noiseSuppression: true,
 486           autoGainControl: true
 487         }
 488       });
 489       videoAcquired = stream.getVideoTracks().length > 0;
 490       audioAcquired = stream.getAudioTracks().length > 0;
 491     } catch (e1) {
 492       console.warn('[MeetingRoom] High-res media profile failed, trying basic video + audio:', e1.message);
 493       // 2. Try basic video + audio
 494       try {
 495         stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ video: true, audio: true });
 496         videoAcquired = stream.getVideoTracks().length > 0;
 497         audioAcquired = stream.getAudioTracks().length > 0;
 498       } catch (e2) {
 499         console.warn('[MeetingRoom] Basic video + audio failed, trying video only:', e2.message);
 500         // 3. Try video only (mic may be locked or permission denied)
 501         try {
 502           stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ video: true, audio: false });
 503           videoAcquired = stream.getVideoTracks().length > 0;
 504           audioAcquired = false;
 505         } catch (e3) {
 506           console.warn('[MeetingRoom] Video only failed, trying audio only:', e3.message);
 507           // 4. Try audio only (camera may be locked or permission denied)
 508           try {
 509             stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ video: false, audio: true });
 510             videoAcquired = false;
 511             audioAcquired = stream.getAudioTracks().length > 0;
 512           } catch (e4) {
 513             console.error('[MeetingRoom] All media device access attempts failed:', e4);
 514             let userMsg = 'Camera and microphone access could not be initialized.';
 515             if (e4.name === 'NotAllowedError' || e4.name === 'PermissionDeniedError') {
 516               userMsg = 'Camera or microphone permission was blocked. Please click the lock or camera icon in your browser address bar to allow access.';
 517             } else if (e4.name === 'NotFoundError' || e4.name === 'DevicesNotFoundError') {
 518               userMsg = 'No camera or microphone found. Please connect a webcam or headset.';
 519             } else if (e4.name === 'NotReadableError' || e4.name === 'TrackStartError') {
 520               userMsg = 'Camera or microphone is already in use by another app (e.g. Zoom, Teams, FaceTime).';
 521             } else if (e4.message) {
 522               userMsg = e4.message;
 523             }
 524 
 525             if (!isMountedRef.current) return null;
 526             setMediaStatus('error');
 527             setMediaError(userMsg);
 528             setVideoOn(false);
 529             return null;
 530           }
 531         }
 532       }
 533     }
 534 
 535     if (!isMountedRef.current) {
 536       if (stream) stream.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop());
 537       return null;
 538     }
 539 
 540     // Enforce default MUTE when entering the meeting room; user must click Unmute to speak
 541     stream.getAudioTracks().forEach(track => {
 542       track.enabled = false;
 543     });
 544     streamRef.current = stream;
 545     setLocalStream(stream);
 546     setMediaStatus('ready');
 547     setVideoOn(videoAcquired);
 548     setAudioOn(false);
 549     audioOnRef.current = false;
 550 
 551     // If active peer calls exist, dynamically replace or attach new tracks
 552     Object.values(callsRef.current).forEach(call => {
 553       const pc = call.peerConnection;
 554       if (pc && typeof pc.getSenders === 'function') {
 555         const senders = pc.getSenders();
 556         stream.getTracks().forEach(track => {
 557           const sender = senders.find(s => s.track && s.track.kind === track.kind);
 558           if (sender) {
 559             try { sender.replaceTrack(track); } catch (e) {}
 560           } else {
 561             try { pc.addTrack(track, stream); } catch (e) {}
 562           }
 563         });
 564       }
 565     });
 566 
 567     return stream;
 568   }, []);
 569 
 570   useEffect(() => {
 571     isMountedRef.current = true;
 572     startMedia();
 573 
 574     return () => {
 575       isMountedRef.current = false;
 576       try {
 577         if (mediaRecorderRef.current && mediaRecorderRef.current.state !== 'inactive') {
 578           mediaRecorderRef.current.stop();
 579         }
 580       } catch {}
 581       if (recordingAudioCtxRef.current) {
 582         try { recordingAudioCtxRef.current.close(); } catch {}
 583         recordingAudioCtxRef.current = null;
 584       }
 585       recordingSourcesRef.current = [];
 586       recordingDestRef.current = null;
 587       if (displayStreamRef.current) {
 588         try { displayStreamRef.current.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop()); } catch {}
 589         displayStreamRef.current = null;
 590       }
 591       if (streamRef.current) {
 592         streamRef.current.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop());
 593       }
 594     };
 595   }, [startMedia]);
 596 
 597   // Robust live video element attachment and playback
 598   useEffect(() => {
 599     const videoEl = localVideoRef.current;
 600     if (!videoEl) return;
 601 
 602     if (!localStream) {
 603       videoEl.srcObject = null;
 604       return;
 605     }
 606 
 607     if (videoEl.srcObject !== localStream) {
 608       videoEl.srcObject = localStream;
 609     }
 610 
 611     // Set muted property directly on DOM element to bypass React JSX muted attribute bug
 612     videoEl.muted = true;
 613     videoEl.defaultMuted = true;
 614     videoEl.playsInline = true;
 615     videoEl.setAttribute('playsinline', '');
 616     videoEl.setAttribute('muted', '');
 617 
 618     const tryPlay = () => {
 619       const p = videoEl.play();
 620       if (p !== undefined) {
 621         p.catch(err => {
 622           console.warn('[MeetingRoom] Autoplay prevented, muting and retrying:', err);
 623           videoEl.muted = true;
 624           videoEl.play().catch(() => {});
 625         });
 626       }
 627     };
 628 
 629     tryPlay();
 630   }, [localStream, videoOn]);
 631 
 632   const setupCall = useCallback((call, defaultName = 'Participant', defaultRole = 'participant') => {
 633     const peerId = call.peer;
 634     callsRef.current[peerId] = call;
 635 
 636     // ── WebRTC RTCPeerConnection Optimization & Resilience ──────────────────
 637     const pc = call.peerConnection;
 638     let statsInterval = null;
 639 
 640     if (pc) {
 641       // 1. Enforce direct sender bitrate capping (500 kbps) & prioritize audio
 642       applySenderConstraints(pc, 500);
 643 
 644       // 2. Automated connection retry triggers if ICE state enters 'failed' or 'disconnected'
 645       pc.oniceconnectionstatechange = () => {
 646         const state = pc.iceConnectionState;
 647         console.log(`[WebRTC ICE Monitor] Peer ${peerId} state:`, state);
 648         if (state === 'disconnected') {
 649           console.warn(`[WebRTC ICE] Disconnected from ${peerId}. Triggering ICE restart recovery...`);
 650           if (typeof pc.restartIce === 'function') {
 651             pc.restartIce();
 652           }
 653         } else if (state === 'failed') {
 654           console.warn(`[WebRTC ICE] Connection failed with ${peerId}. Triggering automated reconnect...`);
 655           if (typeof pc.restartIce === 'function') {
 656             pc.restartIce();
 657           } else if (streamRef.current && peerInstanceRef.current) {
 658             try {
 659               const retryCall = peerInstanceRef.current.call(peerId, streamRef.current, {
 660                 sdpTransform: (sdp) => optimizeSdp(sdp, 500)
 661               });
 662               if (retryCall && setupCallRef.current) setupCallRef.current(retryCall, defaultName, defaultRole);
 663             } catch (retryErr) {
 664               console.warn('[WebRTC ICE] Reconnect call attempt note:', retryErr.message);
 665             }
 666           }
 667         } else if (state === 'connected' || state === 'completed') {
 668           applySenderConstraints(pc, 500);
 669         }
 670       };
 671 
 672       // 3. Automated Resilience Fallback: Heavy Frame Drop Monitoring
 673       let lastFramesReceived = 0;
 674       let lastFramesDropped = 0;
 675       let isThrottled = false;
 676 
 677       statsInterval = setInterval(async () => {
 678         if (!pc || pc.signalingState === 'closed' || !callsRef.current[peerId]) {
 679           clearInterval(statsInterval);
 680           return;
 681         }
 682 
 683         try {
 684           const stats = await pc.getStats();
 685           stats.forEach(report => {
 686             if (report.type === 'inbound-rtp' && report.kind === 'video') {
 687               const currentReceived = report.framesReceived || 0;
 688               const currentDropped = report.framesDropped || 0;
 689               const deltaReceived = currentReceived - lastFramesReceived;
 690               const deltaDropped = currentDropped - lastFramesDropped;
 691 
 692               lastFramesReceived = currentReceived;
 693               lastFramesDropped = currentDropped;
 694 
 695               const totalDelta = deltaReceived + deltaDropped;
 696               if (totalDelta > 15) {
 697                 const dropRatio = deltaDropped / totalDelta;
 698                 if (dropRatio > 0.25 && !isThrottled) {
 699                   // > 25% frames dropped: gracefully downgrade video resolution & bitrate
 700                   console.warn(`[WebRTC Resilience] Heavy frame drop detected (${Math.round(dropRatio * 100)}% drops) for ${peerId}. Gracefully throttling video while preserving uninterrupted high-quality audio calling...`);
 701                   isThrottled = true;
 702                   applySenderConstraints(pc, 250); // Downscale video bitrate to 250kbps
 703                 } else if (dropRatio < 0.08 && isThrottled) {
 704                   // Network connection restored
 705                   console.log(`[WebRTC Resilience] Connection stabilized for ${peerId}. Restoring standard video bitrate...`);
 706                   isThrottled = false;
 707                   applySenderConstraints(pc, 500);
 708                 }
 709               }
 710             }
 711           });
 712         } catch {}
 713       }, 3500);
 714     }
 715 
 716     call.on('stream', (remoteStream) => {
 717       console.log('[PeerJS] Received remote media stream from:', peerId);
 718       remoteStreamsRef.current[peerId] = remoteStream;
 719       upsertPeer(peerId, {
 720         stream: remoteStream,
 721         name: defaultName,
 722         role: defaultRole
 723       });
 724 
 725       // Dynamically attach audio if recording is active
 726       if (recordingAudioCtxRef.current && recordingDestRef.current) {
 727         try {
 728           const audioTracks = remoteStream.getAudioTracks();
 729           if (audioTracks.length > 0) {
 730             const peerAudioStream = new MediaStream([audioTracks[0]]);
 731             const peerSource = recordingAudioCtxRef.current.createMediaStreamSource(peerAudioStream);
 732             peerSource.connect(recordingDestRef.current);
 733             recordingSourcesRef.current.push(peerSource);
 734           }
 735         } catch (e) {
 736           console.warn('[Recorder] Dynamic audio mix attach note:', e);
 737         }
 738       }
 739     });
 740 
 741     call.on('close', () => {
 742       console.log('[PeerJS] Call closed with:', peerId);
 743       if (statsInterval) clearInterval(statsInterval);
 744       removePeer(peerId);
 745     });
 746 
 747     call.on('error', (err) => {
 748       console.warn('[PeerJS] Call error with:', peerId, err);
 749       if (statsInterval) clearInterval(statsInterval);
 750     });
 751   }, [upsertPeer, removePeer]);
 752 
 753   // ── Central Log Aggregator for Live Identity Mapping & Chronological Compilation (BIT 2 & 3) ──
 754   const handleIncomingSpeechChunk = useCallback((chunk) => {
 755     if (!chunk || !chunk.text) return;
 756 
 757     let speakerLabel = '';
 758     if (chunk.isHost) {
 759       speakerLabel = `Host (${chunk.speakerName || 'Host'})`;
 760     } else {
 761       let num = participantOrderRef.current.get(chunk.speakerId);
 762       if (!num) {
 763         const activeIdx = peersRef.current.filter(p => p.id !== '__self__').findIndex(p => p.id === chunk.speakerId);
 764         num = activeIdx >= 0 ? activeIdx + 1 : (participantOrderRef.current.size + 1);
 765         participantOrderRef.current.set(chunk.speakerId, num);
 766       }
 767       speakerLabel = `Participant ${num} (${chunk.speakerName || 'Guest'})`;
 768     }
 769 
 770     const entry = {
 771       id: `${chunk.speakerId || 'spk'}_${chunk.timestamp || Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 6)}`,
 772       timestamp: chunk.timestamp || Date.now(),
 773       speakerId: chunk.speakerId,
 774       speakerLabel,
 775       speakerName: chunk.speakerName,
 776       isHost: Boolean(chunk.isHost),
 777       text: chunk.text
 778     };
 779 
 780     transcriptLogRef.current.push(entry);
 781     transcriptLogRef.current.sort((a, b) => a.timestamp - b.timestamp);
 782     setTranscriptEntries([...transcriptLogRef.current]);
 783   }, []);
 784 
 785   // ── 3. Handle data connection (PeerJS DataConnection) ─────────────────────
 786   const setupDataConnection = useCallback((conn) => {
 787     const peerId = conn.peer;
 788     dataConnsRef.current[peerId] = conn;
 789 
 790     conn.on('open', () => {
 791       try {
 792         conn.send({ type: 'peer_state', key: 'audioOn', value: audioOnRef.current });
 793         conn.send({ type: 'peer_state', key: 'videoOn', value: videoOnRef.current });
 794       } catch (e) {}
 795     });
 796 
 797     conn.on('data', (data) => {
 798       if (!data || !data.type) return;
 799 
 800       switch (data.type) {
 801         // WebRTC Data Channel live speaker text chunk (BIT 2 & BIT 3)
 802         case 'speech_transcript': {
 803           handleIncomingSpeechChunk(data);
 804           break;
 805         }
 806 
 807         // Host receives knock from participant
 808         case 'knock': {
 809           setJoinRequests(prev => {
 810             if (prev.some(r => r.id === peerId)) return prev;
 811             return [...prev, { id: peerId, name: data.name || 'Guest' }];
 812           });
 813           break;
 814         }
 815 
 816         // Guest receives approval from host
 817         case 'knock_approved': {
 818           setIsWaitingForApproval(false);
 819           upsertPeer('__self__', { approved: true });
 820 
 821           // Call host or existing peers
 822           if (data.existingPeers && Array.isArray(data.existingPeers)) {
 823             data.existingPeers.forEach(ep => {
 824               if (ep.id !== peerInstanceRef.current?.id) {
 825                 upsertPeer(ep.id, { name: ep.name, role: ep.role });
 826                 if (streamRef.current && peerInstanceRef.current && !callsRef.current[ep.id]) {
 827                   const call = peerInstanceRef.current.call(ep.id, streamRef.current, {
 828                     sdpTransform: (sdp) => optimizeSdp(sdp, 500)
 829                   });
 830                   if (call) setupCall(call, ep.name, ep.role);
 831                 }
 832               }
 833             });
 834           }
 835           break;
 836         }
 837 
 838         // Guest rejected by host
 839         case 'knock_rejected': {
 840           setIsWaitingForApproval(false);
 841           alert(data.message || 'Host declined your request to join the meeting.');
 842           onEndMeeting();
 843           break;
 844         }
 845 
 846         // Participant notified of another participant joining
 847         case 'participant_joined': {
 848           upsertPeer(data.peerId, { name: data.name, role: 'participant' });
 849           break;
 850         }
 851 
 852         // Audio, Video, Hand-raise state sync
 853         case 'peer_state': {
 854           upsertPeer(peerId, { [data.key]: data.value });
 855           break;
 856         }
 857 
 858         // Participant kicked by host
 859         case 'kicked': {
 860           alert('🚫 You have been removed from the meeting by the host.');
 861           onEndMeeting();
 862           break;
 863         }
 864 
 865         // Live Captions relay
 866         case 'live_caption': {
 867           setLiveCaption({ speaker: data.speaker, text: data.text, wpm: data.wpm });
 868           setTimeout(() => setLiveCaption(null), 4000);
 869           break;
 870         }
 871 
 872         default:
 873           break;
 874       }
 875     });
 876 
 877     conn.on('close', () => {
 878       console.log('[PeerJS] Data connection closed with:', peerId);
 879       removePeer(peerId);
 880     });
 881 
 882     conn.on('error', (err) => {
 883       console.warn('[PeerJS] Data conn error with:', peerId, err);
 884     });
 885   }, [upsertPeer, removePeer, setupCall, onEndMeeting, handleIncomingSpeechChunk]);
 886 
 887   // ── 4. Initialize PeerJS Client ───────────────────────────────────────────
 888   useEffect(() => {
 889     let alive = true;
 890     let peer = null;
 891     let heartbeatInterval = null;
 892 
 893     const initPeer = async () => {
 894       const cleanCode = meetingCode.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '').toUpperCase();
 895       const randomSuffix = Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 7);
 896       const myPeerId = `ge-${isGuest ? 'guest' : 'host'}-${cleanCode}-${randomSuffix}`;
 897 
 898       try {
 899         peer = new Peer(myPeerId, {
 900           ...PEER_OPTIONS,
 901           config: ADVANCED_RTC_CONFIG
 902         });
 903         peerInstanceRef.current = peer;
 904 
 905         peer.on('open', async (id) => {
 906           if (!alive) return;
 907           console.log('[PeerJS] Connected to signaling with ID:', id);
 908           setPeerStatus('open');
 909 
 910           // Keep signaling socket active with periodic heartbeat
 911           if (heartbeatInterval) clearInterval(heartbeatInterval);
 912           heartbeatInterval = setInterval(() => {
 913             if (peer && !peer.destroyed && peer.socket && peer.socket._ws && peer.socket._ws.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
 914               try {
 915                 peer.socket._ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: 'HEARTBEAT' }));
 916               } catch {}
 917             }
 918           }, 15000);
 919 
 920           if (!isGuest) {
 921             // Host creates / registers room in serverless room registry
 922             try {
 923               await roomApi('/api/rooms', {
 924                 method: 'POST',
 925                 headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
 926                 body: JSON.stringify({
 927                   action: 'create',
 928                   code: meetingCode,
 929                   hostPeerId: id,
 930                   hostName: hostName || 'Host',
 931                   topic: meetingName || 'Strategy Sync'
 932                 })
 933               });
 934               setIsWaitingForApproval(false);
 935             } catch (err) {
 936               console.warn('[Room Registry] Error creating room:', err.message);
 937               // Host can proceed even if backend registry had a temporary hiccup
 938               setIsWaitingForApproval(false);
 939             }
 940           } else {
 941             // Guest fetches room details to locate hostPeerId
 942             try {
 943               const res = await roomApi(`/api/rooms/${meetingCode}`, { method: 'GET' });
 944               const hostPeerId = res.hostPeerId || res.room?.hostPeerId;
 945               if (!hostPeerId) {
 946                 alert('Meeting host is not currently in the room. Please check the code.');
 947                 onEndMeeting();
 948                 return;
 949               }
 950 
 951               // Establish DataConnection to host to knock
 952               const conn = peer.connect(hostPeerId, { reliable: true });
 953               conn.on('open', () => {
 954                 conn.send({
 955                   type: 'knock',
 956                   peerId: id,
 957                   name: hostName || 'Guest'
 958                 });
 959               });
 960               setupDataConnection(conn);
 961             } catch (err) {
 962               console.warn('[Room Registry] Join error:', err);
 963               alert('Could not join room. Please check the meeting code.');
 964               onEndMeeting();
 965             }
 966           }
 967         });
 968 
 969         // Listen for incoming data connections
 970         peer.on('connection', (conn) => {
 971           if (!alive) return;
 972           setupDataConnection(conn);
 973         });
 974 
 975         // Listen for incoming media calls
 976         peer.on('call', (call) => {
 977           if (!alive) return;
 978           console.log('[PeerJS] Incoming call from:', call.peer);
 979           // Answer with local media stream if available, applying H264/VP8 codec priority & 500kbps bitrate cap
 980           if (streamRef.current) {
 981             call.answer(streamRef.current, {
 982               sdpTransform: (sdp) => optimizeSdp(sdp, 500)
 983             });
 984           } else {
 985             // Answer with empty audio/video if camera not yet initialized
 986             call.answer(undefined, {
 987               sdpTransform: (sdp) => optimizeSdp(sdp, 500)
 988             });
 989           }
 990           setupCall(call, 'Participant', 'guest');
 991         });
 992 
 993         peer.on('error', (err) => {
 994           console.warn('[PeerJS] Client error:', err);
 995           if (err.type === 'peer-unavailable') {
 996             console.warn('[PeerJS] Target peer unavailable');
 997           } else if (err.type === 'disconnected' || err.type === 'network' || err.type === 'server-error' || err.type === 'socket-error') {
 998             if (!peer.destroyed) {
 999               setPeerStatus('connecting');
1000               setTimeout(() => {
1001                 try {
1002                   if (!peer.destroyed && peer.disconnected) {
1003                     peer.reconnect();
1004                   }
1005                 } catch (recErr) {}
1006               }, 1200);
1007             }
1008           }
1009         });
1010 
1011         peer.on('disconnected', () => {
1012           console.log('[PeerJS] Disconnected from server, attempting reconnect...');
1013           if (!peer.destroyed) {
1014             setPeerStatus('connecting');
1015             setTimeout(() => {
1016               try {
1017                 if (!peer.destroyed && peer.disconnected) {
1018                   peer.reconnect();
1019                 }
1020               } catch (recErr) {
1021                 console.warn('[PeerJS] Reconnect attempt failed:', recErr);
1022               }
1023             }, 1000);
1024           }
1025         });
1026 
1027         peer.on('close', () => {
1028           setPeerStatus('error');
1029         });
1030       } catch (err) {
1031         console.error('[PeerJS] Failed to instantiate Peer:', err);
1032         setPeerStatus('error');
1033       }
1034     };
1035 
1036     initPeer();
1037 
1038     return () => {
1039       alive = false;
1040       if (heartbeatInterval) clearInterval(heartbeatInterval);
1041       Object.values(callsRef.current).forEach(c => { try { c.close(); } catch {} });
1042       Object.values(dataConnsRef.current).forEach(d => { try { d.close(); } catch {} });
1043       callsRef.current = {};
1044       dataConnsRef.current = {};
1045       if (peerInstanceRef.current) {
1046         try { peerInstanceRef.current.destroy(); } catch {}
1047         peerInstanceRef.current = null;
1048       }
1049     };
1050   }, [meetingCode, isGuest, hostName, meetingName, setupCall, setupDataConnection, onEndMeeting]);
1051 
1052   // ── 5. Host Accept & Reject Participant Knock Actions ─────────────────────
1053   const acceptParticipant = useCallback(async (id) => {
1054     // Enforce 5-member limit (Host + 4 participants)
1055     if (peersRef.current.length >= 4) {
1056       alert('Room capacity reached (Maximum 5 members allowed). Cannot admit more participants.');
1057       return;
1058     }
1059 
1060     // Assign deterministic participant number upon admission
1061     if (!participantOrderRef.current.has(id)) {
1062       const nextNum = participantOrderRef.current.size + 1;
1063       participantOrderRef.current.set(id, nextNum);
1064     }
1065 
1066     // 1. Call room API to update registry
1067     try {
1068       await roomApi('/api/rooms', {
1069         method: 'POST',
1070         headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
1071         body: JSON.stringify({
1072           action: 'join',
1073           code: meetingCode,
1074           peerId: id,
1075           name: joinRequests.find(r => r.id === id)?.name || 'Guest'
1076         })
1077       });
1078     } catch (e) {
1079       console.warn('Room join API note:', e);
1080     }
1081 
1082     // 2. Send knock_approved via data connection
1083     const conn = dataConnsRef.current[id];
1084     if (conn && conn.open) {
1085       const existingPeersList = peersRef.current.map(p => ({
1086         id: p.id,
1087         name: p.name,
1088         role: p.role
1089       }));
1090       // Include host in the list
1091       existingPeersList.push({
1092         id: peerInstanceRef.current?.id,
1093         name: hostName || 'Host',
1094         role: 'host'
1095       });
1096 
1097       conn.send({
1098         type: 'knock_approved',
1099         existingPeers: existingPeersList
1100       });
1101     }
1102 
1103     // 3. Initiate media call from Host to Participant with H.264/VP8 & 500kbps bitrate capping
1104     if (peerInstanceRef.current && streamRef.current) {
1105       const call = peerInstanceRef.current.call(id, streamRef.current, {
1106         sdpTransform: (sdp) => optimizeSdp(sdp, 500)
1107       });
1108       if (call) {
1109         const guestName = joinRequests.find(r => r.id === id)?.name || 'Guest';
1110         setupCall(call, guestName, 'participant');
1111       }
1112     }
1113 
1114     // 4. Notify other peers about the new participant
1115     broadcastData({
1116       type: 'participant_joined',
1117       peerId: id,
1118       name: joinRequests.find(r => r.id === id)?.name || 'Guest'
1119     });
1120 
1121     setJoinRequests(prev => prev.filter(r => r.id !== id));
1122   }, [meetingCode, joinRequests, hostName, setupCall, broadcastData]);
1123 
1124   const rejectParticipant = useCallback((id) => {
1125     const conn = dataConnsRef.current[id];
1126     if (conn && conn.open) {
1127       conn.send({
1128         type: 'knock_rejected',
1129         message: 'Host declined your request to join this meeting.'
1130       });
1131       setTimeout(() => {
1132         try { conn.close(); } catch {}
1133       }, 500);
1134     }
1135     setJoinRequests(prev => prev.filter(r => r.id !== id));
1136   }, []);
1137 
1138   // ── 6. Host Kick Participant Actions ──────────────────────────────────────
1139   const requestKick = useCallback((peer) => {
1140     setKickTarget(peer);
1141   }, []);
1142 
1143   const confirmKick = useCallback(() => {
1144     if (!kickTarget) return;
1145     const targetId = kickTarget.id;
1146 
1147     // Send kick message
1148     const conn = dataConnsRef.current[targetId];
1149     if (conn && conn.open) {
1150       conn.send({ type: 'kicked' });
1151     }
1152 
1153     removePeer(targetId);
1154     setKickTarget(null);
1155   }, [kickTarget, removePeer]);
1156 
1157   // ── 7. Control Toggles (Mic, Camera, Hand) ────────────────────────────────
1158   const toggleVideo = useCallback(async () => {
1159     if (!videoOn) {
1160       const currentVideoTracks = streamRef.current?.getVideoTracks() || [];
1161       if (currentVideoTracks.length > 0) {
1162         currentVideoTracks.forEach(t => { t.enabled = true; });
1163         setVideoOn(true);
1164         broadcastData({ type: 'peer_state', key: 'videoOn', value: true });
1165       } else {
1166         await startMedia();
1167       }
1168     } else {
1169       if (streamRef.current) {
1170         streamRef.current.getVideoTracks().forEach(t => { t.enabled = false; });
1171       }
1172       setVideoOn(false);
1173       broadcastData({ type: 'peer_state', key: 'videoOn', value: false });
1174     }
1175   }, [videoOn, broadcastData, startMedia]);
1176 
1177   const toggleAudio = useCallback(async () => {
1178     if (!audioOn) {
1179       const currentAudioTracks = streamRef.current?.getAudioTracks() || [];
1180       if (currentAudioTracks.length > 0) {
1181         currentAudioTracks.forEach(t => { t.enabled = true; });
1182         setAudioOn(true);
1183         broadcastData({ type: 'peer_state', key: 'audioOn', value: true });
1184       } else {
1185         await startMedia();
1186       }
1187     } else {
1188       if (streamRef.current) {
1189         streamRef.current.getAudioTracks().forEach(t => { t.enabled = false; });
1190       }
1191       setAudioOn(false);
1192       broadcastData({ type: 'peer_state', key: 'audioOn', value: false });
1193     }
1194   }, [audioOn, broadcastData, startMedia]);
1195 
1196   const toggleHandRaise = useCallback(() => {
1197     setHandRaised(prev => {
1198       const next = !prev;
1199       broadcastData({
1200         type: 'peer_state',
1201         key: 'handRaised',
1202         value: next
1203       });
1204       return next;
1205     });
1206   }, [broadcastData]);
1207 
1208   // ── 8. Speech Recognition & Telemetry ─────────────────────────────────────
1209   useEffect(() => {
1210     const SR = window.SpeechRecognition || window.webkitSpeechRecognition;
1211     if (!SR || !audioOn) return;
1212 
1213     let recognition;
1214     try {
1215       recognition = new SR();
1216       recognition.continuous = true;
1217       recognition.interimResults = false;
1218       recognition.lang = 'en-US';
1219 
1220       let lastTime = Date.now();
1221       recognition.onresult = (e) => {
1222         const transcript = e.results[e.results.length - 1][0].transcript.trim();
1223         if (!transcript) return;
1224         const words = transcript.split(/\s+/).length;
1225         const now = Date.now();
1226         const minElapsed = (now - lastTime) / 60000;
1227         lastTime = now;
1228         const wpm = minElapsed > 0 ? Math.min(Math.round(words / minElapsed), 220) : 130;
1229         const finalWpm = wpm > 40 ? wpm : 120;
1230 
1231         setLiveCaption({ speaker: hostName || 'You', text: transcript, wpm: finalWpm });
1232         setTimeout(() => setLiveCaption(null), 4000);
1233 
1234         broadcastData({
1235           type: 'live_caption',
1236           speaker: hostName || 'You',
1237           text: transcript,
1238           wpm: finalWpm
1239         });
1240 
1241         // ── WebRTC Data Channel for Live Identity Mapping (BIT 2 & BIT 3) ──
1242         const mySpeakerId = peerInstanceRef.current?.id || 'local_user';
1243         const mySpeakerName = hostName || (isGuest ? 'Guest' : 'Host');
1244 
1245         const speechPayload = {
1246           type: 'speech_transcript',
1247           speakerId: mySpeakerId,
1248           speakerName: mySpeakerName,
1249           isHost: !isGuest,
1250           text: transcript,
1251           timestamp: now
1252         };
1253 
1254         // Broadcast mapped speaker text chunk via WebRTC Data Channels
1255         broadcastData(speechPayload);
1256 
1257         // If this client is Host, aggregate locally into central log
1258         if (!isGuest) {
1259           handleIncomingSpeechChunk(speechPayload);
1260         }
1261       };
1262       recognition.onerror = () => {};
1263       recognition.start();
1264       recognitionRef.current = recognition;
1265     } catch {}
1266 
1267     return () => {
1268       try { if (recognition) recognition.stop(); } catch {}
1269     };
1270   }, [audioOn, hostName, isGuest, broadcastData, handleIncomingSpeechChunk]);
1271 
1272   // ── 9. Active Speaker Energy Detection (Web Audio Analyser) ───────────────
1273   useEffect(() => {
1274     let timer = null;
1275     let audioCtx = null;
1276     let localAnalyser = null;
1277     let localSource = null;
1278     const peerAnalysers = {};
1279 
1280     try {
1281       const AudioCtxClass = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
1282       if (AudioCtxClass) {
1283         audioCtx = new AudioCtxClass();
1284 
1285         if (streamRef.current && streamRef.current.getAudioTracks().length > 0) {
1286           try {
1287             localSource = audioCtx.createMediaStreamSource(streamRef.current);
1288             localAnalyser = audioCtx.createAnalyser();
1289             localAnalyser.fftSize = 256;
1290             localAnalyser.smoothingTimeConstant = 0.3;
1291             localSource.connect(localAnalyser);
1292           } catch (e) {}
1293         }
1294 
1295         Object.entries(remoteStreamsRef.current).forEach(([pid, rStream]) => {
1296           if (rStream && rStream.getAudioTracks().length > 0) {
1297             try {
1298               const src = audioCtx.createMediaStreamSource(rStream);
1299               const ana = audioCtx.createAnalyser();
1300               ana.fftSize = 256;
1301               ana.smoothingTimeConstant = 0.3;
1302               src.connect(ana);
1303               peerAnalysers[pid] = { analyser: ana, source: src };
1304             } catch (e) {}
1305           }
1306         });
1307       }
1308     } catch (e) {}
1309 
1310     const dataArr = new Uint8Array(128);
1311     let lastSwitchTime = Date.now();
1312 
1313     const monitorVolume = () => {
1314       if (!audioCtx) return;
1315       if (audioCtx.state === 'suspended') {
1316         audioCtx.resume().catch(() => {});
1317       }
1318 
1319       let maxVol = 0;
1320       let candidateId = null;
1321 
1322       // 1. Local mic level
1323       if (localAnalyser && audioOnRef.current) {
1324         localAnalyser.getByteFrequencyData(dataArr);
1325         let sum = 0;
1326         for (let i = 0; i < dataArr.length; i++) sum += dataArr[i];
1327         const vol = sum / dataArr.length;
1328         if (vol > 14) {
1329           maxVol = vol;
1330           candidateId = 'local';
1331         }
1332       }
1333 
1334       // 2. Peer mic levels
1335       Object.entries(peerAnalysers).forEach(([pid, obj]) => {
1336         const peer = peersRef.current.find(p => p.id === pid);
1337         if (peer && peer.audioOn !== false) {
1338           obj.analyser.getByteFrequencyData(dataArr);
1339           let sum = 0;
1340           for (let i = 0; i < dataArr.length; i++) sum += dataArr[i];
1341           const vol = sum / dataArr.length;
1342           if (vol > 14 && vol > maxVol) {
1343             maxVol = vol;
1344             candidateId = pid;
1345           }
1346         }
1347       });
1348 
1349       const now = Date.now();
1350       if (candidateId && candidateId !== activeSpeakerIdRef.current) {
1351         if (now - lastSwitchTime > 300) {
1352           activeSpeakerIdRef.current = candidateId;
1353           lastSwitchTime = now;
1354           setActiveSpeakerId(candidateId);
1355 
1356           if (!pinnedSpeakerIdRef.current) {
1357             let name = hostName || 'You';
1358             if (candidateId !== 'local') {
1359               const p = peersRef.current.find(x => x.id === candidateId);
1360               if (p) name = p.name;
1361             }
1362             setActiveSpeakerName(name);
1363             activeSpeakerNameRef.current = name;
1364           }
1365         }
1366       }
1367 
1368       timer = setTimeout(monitorVolume, 100);
1369     };
1370 
1371     timer = setTimeout(monitorVolume, 250);
1372 
1373     return () => {
1374       if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
1375       try {
1376         if (localSource) localSource.disconnect();
1377         Object.values(peerAnalysers).forEach(p => {
1378           try { p.source.disconnect(); } catch {}
1379         });
1380         if (audioCtx) audioCtx.close();
1381       } catch (e) {}
1382     };
1383   }, [peers, audioOn, hostName]);
1384 
1385   // ── 10. Multi-User Full-Screen Grid Recording (BIT 1) ──────────────────────
1386   const toggleRecording = useCallback(async () => {
1387     if (isRecording) {
1388       try {
1389         if (mediaRecorderRef.current && mediaRecorderRef.current.state !== 'inactive') {
1390           mediaRecorderRef.current.stop();
1391         }
1392       } catch (e) {
1393         console.warn('Error stopping recording:', e);
1394       }
1395       if (displayStreamRef.current) {
1396         displayStreamRef.current.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop());
1397         displayStreamRef.current = null;
1398       }
1399       setIsRecording(false);
1400       return;
1401     }
1402 
1403     try {
1404       recordedChunksRef.current = [];
1405 
1406       // 1. Capture Full Screen Layout (contains active grid of up to 5 members)
1407       const displayStream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getDisplayMedia({
1408         video: {
1409           cursor: 'always',
1410           displaySurface: 'browser'
1411         },
1412         audio: true
1413       });
1414       displayStreamRef.current = displayStream;
1415 
1416       // Handle user stopping screen share from browser banner
1417       if (displayStream.getVideoTracks().length > 0) {
1418         displayStream.getVideoTracks()[0].onended = () => {
1419           try {
1420             if (mediaRecorderRef.current && mediaRecorderRef.current.state !== 'inactive') {
1421               mediaRecorderRef.current.stop();
1422             }
1423           } catch (e) {}
1424           setIsRecording(false);
1425         };
1426       }
1427 
1428       // 2. Setup Multi-Source Audio Mixing (Display Audio + Local Mic + Remote Peer Audio)
1429       let destination = null;
1430       let audioCtx = null;
1431       try {
1432         const AudioCtxClass = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
1433         if (AudioCtxClass) {
1434           audioCtx = new AudioCtxClass();
1435           if (audioCtx.state === 'suspended') {
1436             await audioCtx.resume();
1437           }
1438           recordingAudioCtxRef.current = audioCtx;
1439           recordingSourcesRef.current = [];
1440           destination = audioCtx.createMediaStreamDestination();
1441           recordingDestRef.current = destination;
1442 
1443           // A. Display/System Audio
1444           if (displayStream.getAudioTracks().length > 0) {
1445             try {
1446               const displayAudioStream = new MediaStream([displayStream.getAudioTracks()[0]]);
1447               const displaySource = audioCtx.createMediaStreamSource(displayAudioStream);
1448               displaySource.connect(destination);
1449               recordingSourcesRef.current.push(displaySource);
1450             } catch (err) {
1451               console.warn('Display audio mix note:', err);
1452             }
1453           }
1454 
1455           // B. Local Microphone Audio
1456           if (streamRef.current) {
1457             const localAudioTracks = streamRef.current.getAudioTracks();
1458             if (localAudioTracks.length > 0) {
1459               const localAudioStream = new MediaStream([localAudioTracks[0]]);
1460               const localSource = audioCtx.createMediaStreamSource(localAudioStream);
1461               localSource.connect(destination);
1462               recordingSourcesRef.current.push(localSource);
1463             }
1464           }
1465 
1466           // C. Remote Participants Audio
1467           Object.values(remoteStreamsRef.current).forEach(remoteStream => {
1468             if (remoteStream) {
1469               const remoteAudioTracks = remoteStream.getAudioTracks();
1470               if (remoteAudioTracks.length > 0) {
1471                 try {
1472                   const remoteAudioStream = new MediaStream([remoteAudioTracks[0]]);
1473                   const remoteSource = audioCtx.createMediaStreamSource(remoteAudioStream);
1474                   remoteSource.connect(destination);
1475                   recordingSourcesRef.current.push(remoteSource);
1476                 } catch (err) {
1477                   console.warn('Remote stream audio mix note:', err);
1478                 }
1479               }
1480             }
1481           });
1482         }
1483       } catch (mixErr) {
1484         console.warn('Web Audio mix setup note:', mixErr);
1485       }
1486 
1487       // Combine Video Track and Mixed Audio Track
1488       const recordTracks = [displayStream.getVideoTracks()[0]];
1489       if (destination && destination.stream.getAudioTracks().length > 0) {
1490         recordTracks.push(destination.stream.getAudioTracks()[0]);
1491       } else if (displayStream.getAudioTracks().length > 0) {
1492         recordTracks.push(displayStream.getAudioTracks()[0]);
1493       } else if (streamRef.current && streamRef.current.getAudioTracks().length > 0) {
1494         recordTracks.push(streamRef.current.getAudioTracks()[0]);
1495       }
1496 
1497       const recordStream = new MediaStream(recordTracks);
1498 
1499       // Select Best Supported MIME Type
1500       const candidateMimeTypes = [
1501         'video/webm;codecs=vp8,opus',
1502         'video/webm;codecs=vp9,opus',
1503         'video/webm',
1504         'video/mp4'
1505       ];
1506       let selectedMimeType = '';
1507       for (const mime of candidateMimeTypes) {
1508         if (typeof MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported === 'function' && MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported(mime)) {
1509           selectedMimeType = mime;
1510           break;
1511         }
1512       }
1513 
1514       const recorderOptions = {
1515         ...(selectedMimeType ? { mimeType: selectedMimeType } : {}),
1516         audioBitsPerSecond: 128000,
1517         videoBitsPerSecond: 2500000
1518       };
1519 
1520       let recorder;
1521       try {
1522         recorder = new MediaRecorder(recordStream, recorderOptions);
1523       } catch (optErr) {
1524         recorder = new MediaRecorder(recordStream);
1525       }
1526 
1527       recorder.ondataavailable = (e) => {
1528         if (e.data && e.data.size > 0) {
1529           recordedChunksRef.current.push(e.data);
1530         }
1531       };
1532 
1533       recorder.onstop = () => {
1534         if (displayStreamRef.current) {
1535           displayStreamRef.current.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop());
1536           displayStreamRef.current = null;
1537         }
1538 
1539         if (recordedChunksRef.current.length > 0) {
1540           const finalType = selectedMimeType || recorder.mimeType || 'video/webm';
1541           const isMp4 = finalType.includes('mp4');
1542           const ext = isMp4 ? 'mp4' : 'webm';
1543           const blob = new Blob(recordedChunksRef.current, { type: finalType });
1544           const now = new Date();
1545           const elapsedSec = recordingStartTimeRef.current
1546             ? Math.max(1, Math.round((Date.now() - recordingStartTimeRef.current) / 1000))
1547             : 1;
1548           const mins = Math.floor(elapsedSec / 60);
1549           const secs = elapsedSec % 60;
1550           const durationStr = elapsedSec < 60 ? `${elapsedSec}s` : `${mins}m ${secs < 10 ? '0' : ''}${secs}s`;
1551           const filename = `meeting_${meetingCode}_fullscreen_grid_${now.toISOString().slice(0, 10)}_${Date.now().toString().slice(-4)}.${ext}`;
1552 
1553           const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
1554           lastRecordingBlobRef.current = blob;
1555           lastRecordingUrlRef.current = url;
1556           lastRecordingFilenameRef.current = filename;
1557           setRecordedVideoBlob(blob);
1558           setShowGenerateReportBtn(true);
1559 
1560           const recordingItem = {
1561             id: Date.now(),
1562             name: filename,
1563             duration: durationStr,
1564             date: now.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }),
1565             time: now.toLocaleTimeString('en-US', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
1566             size: blob.size,
1567             blob: blob,
1568             meetingCode: meetingCode,
1569             meetingName: `${meetingName || 'Meeting'} (Full Screen Grid)`
1570           };
1571 
1572           saveRecording(recordingItem).catch(err => console.warn('IndexedDB save failed:', err));
1573           if (typeof onSaveRecording === 'function') {
1574             try { onSaveRecording(recordingItem); } catch (e) {}
1575           }
1576 
1577           // Auto-download to user's local downloads
1578           const a = document.createElement('a');
1579           a.href = url;
1580           a.download = filename;
1581           document.body.appendChild(a);
1582           a.click();
1583           document.body.removeChild(a);
1584 
1585           // Upload directly to Apple Silicon M2 GPU Whisper & PyAnnote Diarization Pipeline
1586           const uploadToM2Pipeline = async (videoBlob, videoFilename) => {
1587             setM2ProcessingStatus('Sending full-screen recording to Apple Silicon M2 Whisper & PyAnnote pipeline...');
1588             try {
1589               const formData = new FormData();
1590               formData.append('file', videoBlob, videoFilename);
1591               const liveHost = (hostName && hostName.trim()) ? hostName.trim() : (auth?.currentUser?.displayName || (auth?.currentUser?.email ? auth.currentUser.email.split('@')[0] : 'Host'));
1592               const activePeers = (peers || []).filter(p => p && p.name && p.name !== liveHost && p.id !== '__self__');
1593               const guestNames = activePeers.map(p => p.name).slice(0, 4);
1594               formData.append('speaker_0', liveHost);
1595               formData.append('hostName', liveHost);
1596               guestNames.forEach((gName, idx) => {
1597                 formData.append(`speaker_${idx + 1}`, gName);
1598               });
1599               formData.append('participantNames', JSON.stringify(guestNames));
1600               formData.append('meeting_code', meetingCode || 'MEETING');
1601               formData.append('meetingId', meetingCode || 'MEETING');
1602 
1603               const candidateEndpoints = [
1604                 `${API_BASE_URL}/api/meeting/analyze-video`,
1605                 `${API_BASE_URL}/api/video/diarize-transcript`,
1606                 `http://${typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.location.hostname : 'localhost'}:8000/api/meeting/analyze-video`,
1607                 'http://localhost:8000/api/meeting/analyze-video',
1608                 'http://10.102.167.95:8000/api/meeting/analyze-video',
1609                 'http://localhost:8000/api/video/diarize-transcript'
1610               ].filter((u, i, a) => u && a.indexOf(u) === i);
1611 
1612               let diarData = null;
1613               for (const endpoint of candidateEndpoints) {
1614                 try {
1615                   const res = await fetch(endpoint, {
1616                     method: 'POST',
1617                     body: formData
1618                   });
1619                   if (res.ok) {
1620                     const parsed = await res.json();
1621                     if (parsed && (parsed.data || parsed.rawTranscript || parsed.success)) {
1622                       diarData = parsed.data || parsed;
1623                       break;
1624                     }
1625                   }
1626                 } catch (netErr) {
1627                   console.warn(`[MeetingRoom] Pipeline candidate note (${endpoint}):`, netErr.message);
1628                 }
1629               }
1630 
1631               if (diarData) {
1632                 setM2ScriptResult(diarData);
1633                 setM2ProcessingStatus(`✅ M2 GPU Diarization & Whisper Complete (${diarData.execution_time_seconds || 1.2}s)`);
1634                 setShowScriptModal(true);
1635                 if (typeof onSaveRecording === 'function') {
1636                   try {
1637                     onSaveRecording({
1638                       ...recordingItem,
1639                       scriptText: diarData.scriptText,
1640                       scriptDownloadUrl: diarData.download_url
1641                     });
1642                   } catch (e) {}
1643                 }
1644                 setTimeout(() => setM2ProcessingStatus(null), 5000);
1645               } else {
1646                 setM2ProcessingStatus(null);
1647               }
1648             } catch (pipelineErr) {
1649               console.error('M2 Diarization pipeline error:', pipelineErr);
1650               setM2ProcessingStatus(null);
1651             }
1652           };
1653 
1654           uploadToM2Pipeline(blob, filename);
1655         }
1656 
1657         if (recordingAudioCtxRef.current) {
1658           try { recordingAudioCtxRef.current.close(); } catch {}
1659           recordingAudioCtxRef.current = null;
1660         }
1661         recordingSourcesRef.current = [];
1662         recordingDestRef.current = null;
1663         setIsRecording(false);
1664       };
1665 
1666       recordingStartTimeRef.current = Date.now();
1667       recorder.start(1000);
1668       mediaRecorderRef.current = recorder;
1669       setIsRecording(true);
1670     } catch (err) {
1671       if (err.name !== 'NotAllowedError' && err.name !== 'AbortError') {
1672         console.error('Screen recorder error:', err);
1673         alert('Could not start screen recording: ' + err.message);
1674       }
1675       if (displayStreamRef.current) {
1676         displayStreamRef.current.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop());
1677         displayStreamRef.current = null;
1678       }
1679       if (recordingAudioCtxRef.current) {
1680         try { recordingAudioCtxRef.current.close(); } catch {}
1681         recordingAudioCtxRef.current = null;
1682       }
1683       recordingSourcesRef.current = [];
1684       recordingDestRef.current = null;
1685       setIsRecording(false);
1686     }
1687   }, [isRecording, meetingCode, meetingName, onSaveRecording]);
1688 
1689   // ── Firebase Storage Upload & Report Generation ────────────────────────────
1690   const handleGenerateReport = async () => {
1691     const blobToUpload = recordedVideoBlob || lastRecordingBlobRef.current;
1692     if (!blobToUpload) {
1693       alert("No recorded video found to upload.");
1694       return;
1695     }
1696 
1697     const currentUser = getCurrentAuthUser();
1698     const userId = currentUser?.uid || auth?.currentUser?.uid || `user_${(hostName || 'host').toLowerCase()}`;
1699     const cleanMeetingId = meetingCode || `meeting_${Date.now()}`;
1700     const file = new File([blobToUpload], `${cleanMeetingId}.mp4`, { type: 'video/mp4' });
1701 
1702     setIsUploadingToFirebase(true);
1703     setFirebaseUploadProgress(0);
1704     setFirebaseUploadStatus(`Preparing upload to meetings/${userId}/${cleanMeetingId}.mp4...`);
1705 
1706     try {
1707       const result = await uploadMeetingVideoToFirebase(
1708         file,
1709         userId,
1710         cleanMeetingId,
1711         (progress) => {
1712           setFirebaseUploadProgress(progress);
1713           setFirebaseUploadStatus(`Uploading to Firebase Storage: ${progress}% • meetings/${userId}/${cleanMeetingId}.mp4`);
1714         }
1715       );
1716 
1717       setFirebaseUploadProgress(100);
1718       setFirebaseUploadStatus(`✅ Successfully uploaded to meetings/${userId}/${cleanMeetingId}.mp4`);
1719 
1720       // Notify parent recording handler if available
1721       if (typeof onSaveRecording === 'function' && lastRecordingFilenameRef.current) {
1722         try {
1723           onSaveRecording({
1724             id: Date.now(),
1725             name: lastRecordingFilenameRef.current,
1726             blob: blobToUpload,
1727             meetingCode: cleanMeetingId,
1728             videoStorageUrl: result.downloadUrl,
1729             storagePath: result.storagePath
1730           });
1731         } catch (e) {}
1732       }
1733 
1734       setTimeout(() => {
1735         setIsUploadingToFirebase(false);
1736       }, 4500);
1737     } catch (err) {
1738       console.error('[Firebase Storage] Upload error:', err);
1739       setFirebaseUploadStatus(`Upload failed: ${err.message}`);
1740       setTimeout(() => {
1741         setIsUploadingToFirebase(false);
1742       }, 5000);
1743     }
1744   };
1745 
1746   const handleNotesChange = (e) => {
1747     const text = e.target.value;
1748     setNotesValue(text);
1749     try { localStorage.setItem(`guardianeye_notes_${meetingCode}`, text); } catch {}
1750   };
1751 
1752   const getShareLink = useCallback(() => {
1753     return `${window.location.origin}/?join=${meetingCode}`;
1754   }, [meetingCode]);
1755 
1756   const copyCode = useCallback(() => {
1757     const link = getShareLink();
1758     navigator.clipboard.writeText(link).catch(() => navigator.clipboard.writeText(meetingCode).catch(() => {}));
1759     setCodeCopied(true);
1760     setTimeout(() => setCodeCopied(false), 2500);
1761   }, [getShareLink, meetingCode]);
1762 
1763   const handleEndMeeting = useCallback(() => {
1764     try {
1765       if (mediaRecorderRef.current && mediaRecorderRef.current.state !== 'inactive') {
1766         mediaRecorderRef.current.stop();
1767       }
1768     } catch {}
1769 
1770     if (displayStreamRef.current) {
1771       displayStreamRef.current.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop());
1772       displayStreamRef.current = null;
1773     }
1774     if (recordingAudioCtxRef.current) {
1775       try { recordingAudioCtxRef.current.close(); } catch {}
1776       recordingAudioCtxRef.current = null;
1777     }
1778     recordingSourcesRef.current = [];
1779     recordingDestRef.current = null;
1780     streamRef.current?.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop());
1781     try { recognitionRef.current?.stop(); } catch {}
1782 
1783     Object.values(callsRef.current).forEach(c => { try { c.close(); } catch {} });
1784     Object.values(dataConnsRef.current).forEach(d => { try { d.close(); } catch {} });
1785     callsRef.current = {};
1786     dataConnsRef.current = {};
1787 
1788     if (peerInstanceRef.current) {
1789       try { peerInstanceRef.current.destroy(); } catch {}
1790       peerInstanceRef.current = null;
1791     }
1792 
1793     // ── Compile Chronological Conversation Movie Script (BIT 3) ──
1794     const dateStr = new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' });
1795     const compiledScript = compileMovieScript(transcriptLogRef.current, meetingName, dateStr);
1796 
1797     const elapsedSec = recordingStartTimeRef.current
1798       ? Math.max(1, Math.round((Date.now() - recordingStartTimeRef.current) / 1000))
1799       : 0;
1800     const mins = Math.floor(elapsedSec / 60);
1801     const secs = elapsedSec % 60;
1802     const durationStr = elapsedSec > 0
1803       ? (elapsedSec < 60 ? `${elapsedSec}s` : `${mins}m ${secs < 10 ? '0' : ''}${secs}s`)
1804       : 'Session Completed';
1805 
1806     // Hand off meeting recording and script assets to parent App
1807     if (typeof onEndMeeting === 'function') {
1808       onEndMeeting({
1809         recordingBlob: lastRecordingBlobRef.current,
1810         recordingUrl: lastRecordingUrlRef.current,
1811         recordingFilename: lastRecordingFilenameRef.current,
1812         scriptText: compiledScript,
1813         transcriptsList: [...transcriptLogRef.current],
1814         meetingName: meetingName || 'Strategy Sync',
1815         meetingCode: meetingCode,
1816         duration: durationStr,
1817         hostName: hostName || 'Host',
1818         participantNames: peersRef.current.filter(p => p.id !== '__self__' && p.name).map(p => p.name),
1819         participantsCount: 1 + peersRef.current.filter(p => p.id !== '__self__').length,
1820         isGuest: isGuest
1821       });
1822     }
1823   }, [meetingCode, meetingName, hostName, isGuest, onEndMeeting]);
1824 
1825   // ── Render ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
1826   const visiblePeers = peers.filter(p => p.id !== '__self__');
1827   const totalMembers = 1 + visiblePeers.length;
1828   const gridClass = `grid-${Math.min(totalMembers, 6)}`;
1829 
1830   return (
1831     <div className="meeting-room">
1832       {/* Top Bar */}
1833       <div className="meeting-code-overlay">
1834         <div className="meeting-code-badge" onClick={copyCode} title="Copy invite link">
1835           <span className="code-label">{codeCopied ? 'Link Copied!' : 'Meeting Code'}</span>
1836           <span className="code-value">{meetingCode}</span>
1837           {codeCopied ? <Check size={14} className="code-copy-icon" /> : <Copy size={14} className="code-copy-icon" />}
1838         </div>
1839 
1840         <div className="meeting-topic-badge">{meetingName || 'Strategy Sync'}</div>
1841 
1842         <div className="capacity-badge">
1843           <Users size={14} />
1844           <span>{totalMembers} / 5 {isGuest ? '(Guest)' : '(Host)'}</span>
1845         </div>
1846 
1847         {/* Peer Connection Status Indicator */}
1848         {peerStatus !== 'open' && peers.filter(p => p.id !== '__self__').length === 0 && (
1849           <div style={{
1850             display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '5px',
1851             padding: '4px 10px', borderRadius: '20px',

2232                   className="m2-script-btn m2-copy-btn"
2233                   onClick={() => {
2234                     if (m2ScriptResult.scriptText) {
2235                       navigator.clipboard.writeText(m2ScriptResult.scriptText);
2236                       setScriptCopied(true);
2237                       setTimeout(() => setScriptCopied(false), 2000);
2238                     }
2239                   }}
2240                 >
2241                   {scriptCopied ? <Check size={14} /> : <Copy size={14} />}
2242                   <span>{scriptCopied ? 'Copied' : 'Copy Text'}</span>
2243                 </button>
2244 
2245                 <a
2246                   href={`http://${typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.location.hostname : 'localhost'}:8000${m2ScriptResult.download_url || `/api/transcription/download/${m2ScriptResult.videoId}`}`}
2247                   download={m2ScriptResult.scriptFilename || "meeting_dialogue_script.txt"}
2248                   className="m2-script-btn m2-download-btn"
2249                 >
2250                   <Download size={14} />
2251                   <span>Download .txt Script</span>
2252                 </a>
2253               </div>
2254             </div>
2255           </div>
2256         </div>
2257       )}
2258     </div>
2259   );
2260 }
