  92 function generateUniqueMeetingId() {
  93   const randomSuffix = typeof crypto !== 'undefined' && crypto.randomUUID
  94     ? crypto.randomUUID().slice(0, 8)
  95     : Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 10);
  96   return `meeting_${Date.now()}_${randomSuffix}`;
  97 }
  98 
  99 export default function MeetingDashboard({
 100   meetingId,
 101   initialData = null,
 102   videoTitle = '',
 103   hostName: propHostName = '',
 104   guestName: propGuestName = '',
 105   onBack
 106 }) {
 107   // 1. Dynamic Meeting ID Generation Fix:
 108   // Completely remove the static/hardcoded ID 'meeting_default'.
 109   // Generate a unique random ID for every session.
 110   const [activeMeetingId, setActiveMeetingId] = useState(() => {
 111     const raw = meetingId || initialData?.meetingId || initialData?.meetingCode;
 112     if (raw && raw !== 'meeting_default' && raw !== 'default') {
 113       return String(raw);
 114     }
 115     return generateUniqueMeetingId();
 116   });
 117 
 118   const cleanMeetingId = activeMeetingId;
 119 
 120   // Local component states initialized cleanly for the active meeting
 121   const [meetingDoc, setMeetingDoc] = useState(() => {
 122     if (initialData && initialData.meetingId === cleanMeetingId && initialData.meetingId !== 'meeting_default') {
 123       return initialData;
 124     }
 125     return { meetingId: cleanMeetingId };
 126   });
 127   const [transcriptDoc, setTranscriptDoc] = useState({});
 128   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
 129   const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
 130   const [selectedAuditFilter, setSelectedAuditFilter] = useState('all');
 131   const [copiedTranscript, setCopiedTranscript] = useState(false);
 132   const [isAuditing, setIsAuditing] = useState(false);
 133   const [isExportingPdf, setIsExportingPdf] = useState(false);
 134   const [auditNotice, setAuditNotice] = useState('');
 135   const reportContainerRef = useRef(null);
 136   const dialogueEndRef = useRef(null);
 137 
 138   const [currentStep, setCurrentStep] = useState(1);
 139   const [transcriptionStatus, setTranscriptionStatus] = useState(() => {
 140     return initialData?.transcriptionStatus || initialData?.ratingsStatus || 'idle';
 141   });
 142 
 143   const [isUploadingVideo, setIsUploadingVideo] = useState(false);
 144   const [uploadProgress, setUploadProgress] = useState(0);
 145   const [uploadStatus, setUploadStatus] = useState('');
 146   const fileInputRef = useRef(null);
 147 
 148   // Sync when parent prop changes to a new non-default meeting ID or when initialData arrives
 149   useEffect(() => {
 150     if (meetingId && meetingId !== 'meeting_default' && meetingId !== activeMeetingId) {
 151       setActiveMeetingId(String(meetingId));
 152       if (initialData && (initialData.dialogueTurns?.length > 0 || initialData.rawTranscript || initialData.speakerRatings)) {
 153         setMeetingDoc(prev => ({
 154           ...prev,
 155           meetingId: String(meetingId),
 156           ...initialData,
 157           transcriptionStatus: initialData.transcriptionStatus || 'completed',
 158           ratingsStatus: initialData.ratingsStatus || 'completed',
 159           factCheckStatus: initialData.factCheckStatus || 'completed'
 160         }));
 161         setTranscriptDoc(initialData.transcriptDoc || initialData);
 162         setTranscriptionStatus('completed');
 163         setLoading(false);
 164       } else {
 165         // Absolute Cache Purge
 166         setMeetingDoc({
 167           meetingId: String(meetingId),
 168           rawTranscript: null,
 169           transcriptText: null,
 170           dialogueTurns: [],
 171           segments: [],
 172           speakerRatings: null,
 173           factCheckAudit: [],
 174           summary: null,
 175           meetingSummary: null,
 176           transcriptionStatus: 'processing',
 177           ratingsStatus: 'processing',
 178           factCheckStatus: 'processing'
 179         });
 180         setTranscriptDoc({});
 181         setCurrentStep(1);
 182         setTranscriptionStatus('processing');
 183         setLoading(true);
 184       }
 185     }
 186   }, [meetingId, activeMeetingId, initialData]);
 187 
 188   // Keep state synced whenever initialData updates
 189   useEffect(() => {
 190     if (initialData && (initialData.dialogueTurns?.length > 0 || initialData.rawTranscript || initialData.speakerRatings)) {
 191       setMeetingDoc(prev => ({
 192         ...prev,
 193         ...initialData,
 194         meetingId: initialData.meetingId || activeMeetingId,
 195         transcriptionStatus: initialData.transcriptionStatus || 'completed',
 196         ratingsStatus: initialData.ratingsStatus || 'completed',
 197         factCheckStatus: initialData.factCheckStatus || 'completed'
 198       }));
 199       setTranscriptDoc(initialData.transcriptDoc || initialData);
 200       setTranscriptionStatus('completed');
 201       setLoading(false);
 202     }
 203   }, [initialData, activeMeetingId]);
 204 
 205   // Dynamic Identity Injection:
 206   // Capture authenticated user identity at session moment and roster metadata
 207   const currentAuth = getCurrentAuthUser() || auth?.currentUser;
 208   const authUserName = currentAuth?.displayName || (currentAuth?.email ? currentAuth.email.split('@')[0] : null);
 209 
 210   const hostName = useMemo(() => {
 211     const candidate =
 212       meetingDoc?.hostName ||
 213       initialData?.hostName ||
 214       propHostName ||
 215       authUserName ||
 216       'Host';
 217     return (candidate && candidate.trim()) ? candidate.trim() : (authUserName || 'Host');
 218   }, [meetingDoc, initialData, propHostName, authUserName]);
 219 
 220   const guestName = useMemo(() => {
 221     const candidate =
 222       meetingDoc?.guestName ||
 223       (Array.isArray(meetingDoc?.participantNames) && meetingDoc.participantNames[0]) ||
 224       (meetingDoc?.participants && (meetingDoc.participants[0]?.name || meetingDoc.participants[0])) ||
 225       (Array.isArray(initialData?.participantNames) && initialData.participantNames[0]) ||
 226       initialData?.guestName ||
 227       propGuestName ||
 228       'Participant';
 229     return (candidate && candidate.trim() && candidate !== 'Guest') ? candidate.trim() : 'Participant';
 230   }, [meetingDoc, initialData, propGuestName]);
 231 
 232   // 1. Real-time Firestore Listeners: Track meetings/{cleanMeetingId} & transcripts/{cleanMeetingId}
 233   // As soon as transcriptionStatus or ratingsStatus switches to 'completed', instantly clear loading
 234   useEffect(() => {
 235     if (!cleanMeetingId) return;
 236 
 237     let isSubscribed = true;
 238 
 239     // Initial snapshot fetch
 240     fetchMeetingDocument(cleanMeetingId).then((data) => {
 241       if (!isSubscribed) return;
 242       if (data && (!data.meetingId || data.meetingId === cleanMeetingId)) {
 243         setMeetingDoc((prev) => ({ ...prev, ...data }));
 244         if (data.transcriptionStatus === 'completed' || data.ratingsStatus === 'completed' || data.transcriptStatus === 'completed') {
 245           setCurrentStep(4);
 246           setTranscriptionStatus('completed');
 247           setLoading(false);
 248           setIsUploadingVideo(false);
 249         }
 250       }
 251     }).catch(() => {}).finally(() => {
 252       if (isSubscribed && (transcriptionStatus === 'completed' || meetingDoc?.transcriptionStatus === 'completed')) {
 253         setLoading(false);
 254       }
 255     });
 256 
 257     fetchMeetingTranscript(cleanMeetingId).then((data) => {
 258       if (!isSubscribed) return;
 259       if (data && (!data.meetingId || data.meetingId === cleanMeetingId)) {
 260         setTranscriptDoc((prev) => ({ ...prev, ...data }));
 261         if (data.status === 'completed') {
 262           setCurrentStep(4);
 263           setTranscriptionStatus('completed');
 264           setLoading(false);
 265           setIsUploadingVideo(false);
 266         }
 267       }
 268     }).catch(() => {});
 269 
 270     // Subscribe in real-time to meetings collection for this specific dynamic ID
 271     const unsubMeeting = subscribeToMeetingDocument(cleanMeetingId, (updated) => {
 272       if (!isSubscribed) return;
 273       if (updated && (!updated.meetingId || updated.meetingId === cleanMeetingId)) {
 274         setMeetingDoc((prev) => ({ ...prev, ...updated }));
 275         // As soon as transcriptionStatus or ratingsStatus switches to 'completed', instantly clear loading
 276         if (updated.transcriptionStatus === 'completed' || updated.ratingsStatus === 'completed' || updated.transcriptStatus === 'completed') {
 277           setCurrentStep(4);
 278           setTranscriptionStatus('completed');
 279           setLoading(false);
 280           setIsUploadingVideo(false);
 281         }
 282       }
 283     });
 284 
 285     // Subscribe in real-time to transcripts collection for this specific dynamic ID
 286     const unsubTranscript = subscribeToTranscript(cleanMeetingId, (updated) => {
 287       if (!isSubscribed) return;
 288       if (updated && (!updated.meetingId || updated.meetingId === cleanMeetingId)) {
 289         setTranscriptDoc((prev) => ({ ...prev, ...updated }));
 290         if (updated.status === 'completed') {
 291           setCurrentStep(4);
 292           setTranscriptionStatus('completed');
 293           setLoading(false);
 294           setIsUploadingVideo(false);
 295         }
 296       }
 297     });
 298 
 299     return () => {
 300       isSubscribed = false;
 301       unsubMeeting();
 302       unsubTranscript();
 303     };
 304   }, [cleanMeetingId]);
 305 
 306   const isProcessing = useMemo(() => {
 307     // Instantly clear loading overlay view entirely and unmount spinner component if completed
 308     const mTransStatus = transcriptionStatus || meetingDoc?.transcriptionStatus;
 309     const mRateStatus = meetingDoc?.ratingsStatus;
 310     const mDocStatus = meetingDoc?.transcriptStatus;
 311     const tDocStatus = transcriptDoc?.status;
 312 
 313     const isCompleted =
 314       mTransStatus === 'completed' ||
 315       mRateStatus === 'completed' ||
 316       mDocStatus === 'completed' ||
 317       tDocStatus === 'completed';
 318 
 319     if (isCompleted) {
 320       return false;
 321     }
 322 
 323     if (mTransStatus === 'failed' || mRateStatus === 'failed') {
 324       return false;
 325     }
 326 
 327     // If actively uploading video or running pipeline
 328     if (isUploadingVideo || loading) return true;
 329 
 330     if (mTransStatus === 'processing' || mTransStatus === 'analyzing' || tDocStatus === 'processing') {
 331       return true;
 332     }
 333 
 334     // If session is idle or awaiting video selection, do not block UI with spinner
 335     if (mTransStatus === 'idle' || !mTransStatus) {
 336       return false;
 337     }
 338 
 339     return false;
 340   }, [meetingDoc, transcriptDoc, loading, isUploadingVideo, transcriptionStatus]);
 341   const handleUploadAndAnalyze = async (e) => {
 342     const file = e.target?.files?.[0];
 343     if (!file) return;
 344 
 345     // 1. Every new video selected triggers a unique dynamic session ID using a timestamp suffix
 346     const newSessionId = `meeting_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 8)}`;
 347     setActiveMeetingId(newSessionId);
 348 
 349     // 2. Absolute Cache Purge & Force Sync:
 350     setMeetingDoc({
 351       meetingId: newSessionId,
 352       rawTranscript: null,
 353       transcriptText: null,
 354       dialogueTurns: [],
 355       segments: [],
 356       speakerRatings: null,
 357       factCheckAudit: [],
 358       summary: null,
 359       meetingSummary: null,
 360       transcriptionStatus: 'processing',
 361       ratingsStatus: 'processing',
 362       factCheckStatus: 'processing'
 363     });
 364     setTranscriptDoc({
 365       meetingId: newSessionId,
 366       status: 'processing',
 367       dialogueTurns: [],
 368       rawTranscript: null
 369     });
 370     setLoading(true);
 371     setIsUploadingVideo(true);
 372     setCurrentStep(1);
 373     setTranscriptionStatus('processing');
 374     setUploadProgress(15);
 375     setUploadStatus('Step 1/3: Extracting 16kHz audio from video file...');
 376 
 377     const sessionHostIdentity = (hostName && hostName.trim()) ? hostName.trim() : 'Host';
 378     const sessionGuestIdentity = guestName || 'Participant 1';
 379 
 380     try {
 381       setUploadStatus('Step 2/3: Gemini 2.5 Flash Native Audio transcribing & diarizing...');
 382       const backendRes = await analyzeVideoWithGroqAndGemini(file, {
 383         meetingId: newSessionId,
 384         hostName: sessionHostIdentity,
 385         participantNames: [sessionGuestIdentity],
 386         onProgress: (pct) => {
 387           setUploadProgress(pct);
 388           if (pct >= 90) {
 389             setCurrentStep(3);
 390             setUploadStatus('Step 3/3: Gemini 2.5 Flash scoring & fact-checking...');
 391           } else if (pct >= 30) {
 392             setCurrentStep(2);
 393             setUploadStatus('Step 2/3: Gemini 2.5 Flash Native Audio transcribing & diarizing...');
 394           }
 395         }
 396       });
 397 
 398       if (backendRes) {
 399         if (backendRes.success === false) {
 400           setCurrentStep(1);
 401           setIsUploadingVideo(false);
 402           setLoading(false);
 403           setTranscriptionStatus('failed');
 404           setUploadStatus(`❌ Error: ${backendRes.error || backendRes.detail || 'Analysis could not be generated.'}`);
 405           return;
 406         }
 407 
 408         setCurrentStep(4);
 409         setTranscriptionStatus('completed');
 410         setIsUploadingVideo(false);
 411         setLoading(false);
 412         setUploadProgress(100);
 413         setMeetingDoc((prev) => ({
 414           ...prev,
 415           ...backendRes,
 416           meetingId: newSessionId,
 417           transcriptionStatus: 'completed',
 418           ratingsStatus: 'completed',
 419           factCheckStatus: 'completed'
 420         }));
 421         setTranscriptDoc((prev) => ({
 422           ...prev,
 423           ...backendRes,
 424           meetingId: newSessionId,
 425           status: 'completed'
 426         }));
 427         setUploadStatus('✅ Analysis completed: 100% media-driven verbatim pipeline.');
 428       }
 429     } catch (err) {
 430       console.error('[MeetingDashboard] Upload error or network exception:', err);
 431       setIsUploadingVideo(false);
 432       setLoading(false);
 433       setTranscriptionStatus('failed');
 434       setUploadStatus(`❌ Analysis Error: ${err.message || 'Failed to process video file.'}`);
 435     } finally {
 436       if (fileInputRef.current) fileInputRef.current.value = '';
 437     }
 438   };
 439 
 440   // Extract rawTranscript string
 441   const rawTranscriptText = useMemo(() => {
 442     return (
 443       meetingDoc?.rawTranscript ||
 444       transcriptDoc?.rawTranscript ||
 445       transcriptDoc?.transcriptText ||
 446       meetingDoc?.transcriptText ||
 447       initialData?.rawTranscript ||
 448       initialData?.transcriptText ||
 449       ''
 450     );
 451   }, [meetingDoc, transcriptDoc, initialData]);
 452 
 453   // Parse verbatim dialogue turns with multiple fallback sources:
 454   const dialogueTurns = useMemo(() => {
 455     // 1. Check meetingDoc dialogueTurns
 456     if (meetingDoc?.dialogueTurns && Array.isArray(meetingDoc.dialogueTurns) && meetingDoc.dialogueTurns.length > 0) {
 457       return meetingDoc.dialogueTurns;
 458     }
 459     // 2. Check transcriptDoc dialogueTurns
 460     if (transcriptDoc?.dialogueTurns && Array.isArray(transcriptDoc.dialogueTurns) && transcriptDoc.dialogueTurns.length > 0) {
 461       return transcriptDoc.dialogueTurns;
 462     }
 463     // 3. Check initialData dialogueTurns
 464     if (initialData?.dialogueTurns && Array.isArray(initialData.dialogueTurns) && initialData.dialogueTurns.length > 0) {
 465       return initialData.dialogueTurns;
 466     }
 467 
 468     // 4. Check segments array in any of the docs
 469     const rawSegments = meetingDoc?.segments || transcriptDoc?.segments || initialData?.segments;
 470     if (Array.isArray(rawSegments) && rawSegments.length > 0) {
 471       return rawSegments.map((seg, idx) => {
 472         const text = seg.text || seg.transcript || '';
 473         const spk = seg.speaker || seg.speaker_name || (idx % 2 === 0 ? hostName : guestName);
 474         const isHost = spk.toLowerCase().includes(hostName.toLowerCase()) || idx % 2 === 0;
 475         return {
 476           id: seg.id || `turn_${idx}`,
 477           speaker: spk,
 478           role: isHost ? 'Host' : 'Participant',
 479           isHost,
 480           text,
 481           timestamp: seg.timestamp || `00:${String(idx * 4).padStart(2, '0')}`
 482         };
 483       });
 484     }
 485 
 486     // 5. Parse from raw transcript text
 487     if (rawTranscriptText && typeof rawTranscriptText === 'string' && rawTranscriptText.trim()) {
 488       const lines = rawTranscriptText.split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim().length > 0);
 489       const parsed = lines.map((line, idx) => {
 490         const match = line.match(/^([^:]+):\s*(.+)$/);
 491         const speaker = match ? match[1].trim() : hostName;
 492         const text = match ? match[2].trim() : line.trim();
 493         const isHost = speaker.toLowerCase() === hostName.toLowerCase() || speaker.toLowerCase().includes('host');
 494 
 495         return {
 496           id: `turn_${idx}`,
 497           speaker,
 498           isHost,
 499           role: isHost ? 'Host' : 'Participant',
 500           text,
 501           timestamp: `00:${String(idx * 4).padStart(2, '0')}`
 502         };
 503       });
 504       if (parsed.length > 0) return parsed;
 505     }
 506 
 507     return [];
 508   }, [meetingDoc, transcriptDoc, initialData, rawTranscriptText]);
 509 
 510   // Filter dialogue turns based on search query
 511   const filteredDialogue = useMemo(() => {
 512     if (!searchQuery.trim()) return dialogueTurns;
 513     const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
 514     return dialogueTurns.filter(
 515       (turn) => turn.speaker.toLowerCase().includes(q) || turn.text.toLowerCase().includes(q)
 516     );
 517   }, [dialogueTurns, searchQuery]);
 518 
 519   // Extract Speaker Ratings (5 parameters scored 1-10)
 520   // Strictly conditional: if participant did NOT speak, their key is omitted from Firestore and the UI.
 521   const speakerRatings = useMemo(() => {
 522     const source = meetingDoc?.speakerRatings || transcriptDoc?.speakerRatings || initialData?.speakerRatings;
 523     if (source && typeof source === 'object' && Object.keys(source).length > 0) {
 524       const parsed = {};
 525       for (const [spk, metrics] of Object.entries(source)) {
 526         if (metrics && typeof metrics === 'object') {
 527           parsed[spk] = {
 528             communication_skills: Math.max(1, Math.min(10, Number(metrics.communication_skills) || 5)),
 529             fluency: Math.max(1, Math.min(10, Number(metrics.fluency) || 5)),
 530             confidence: Math.max(1, Math.min(10, Number(metrics.confidence) || 5)),
 531             awkwardness: Math.max(1, Math.min(10, Number(metrics.awkwardness) || 3)),
 532             straightforward_answers: Math.max(1, Math.min(10, Number(metrics.straightforward_answers) || 5)),
 533             persuasive_assertiveness: Math.max(1, Math.min(10, Number(metrics.persuasive_assertiveness) || Number(metrics.confidence) || 5)),
 534             tone_style: metrics.tone_style ? String(metrics.tone_style).trim() : 'Collaborative',
 535             rationale: metrics.rationale,
 536             wpm: metrics.wpm || 0,
 537             filler_count: metrics.filler_count !== undefined ? metrics.filler_count : (metrics.fillers || 0),
 538             word_count: metrics.word_count || 0,
 539             char_count: metrics.char_count || 0,
 540             segment_count: metrics.segment_count || 0,
 541             is_clamped: Boolean(metrics.is_clamped),
 542             status_note: metrics.status_note
 543           };
 544         }
 545       }
 546       if (Object.keys(parsed).length > 0) {
 547         return parsed;
 548       }
 549     }
 550 
 551     return {};
 552   }, [meetingDoc, transcriptDoc, initialData]);
 553 
 554   // Extract Fact-Checking Audit array: strictly from backend / Firestore
 555   // [ { question_by, question, answered_by, answer, verdict: "Accurate" | "Incomplete" | "Evasive" } ]
 556   const factCheckAudit = useMemo(() => {
 557     const rawAudit =
 558       meetingDoc?.factCheckAudit ||
 559       transcriptDoc?.factCheckAudit ||
 560       initialData?.factCheckAudit;
 561 
 562     if (Array.isArray(rawAudit) && rawAudit.length > 0) {
 563       return rawAudit;
 564     }
 565 
 566     return [];
 567   }, [meetingDoc, transcriptDoc, initialData]);
 568 
 569   // Filter audit questions by selected user
 570   const filteredAudit = useMemo(() => {
 571     if (selectedAuditFilter === 'all') return factCheckAudit;
 572     const filterLower = selectedAuditFilter.toLowerCase();
 573     return factCheckAudit.filter(
 574       (a) => (a.question_by && a.question_by.toLowerCase() === filterLower) ||
 575              (a.answered_by && a.answered_by.toLowerCase() === filterLower)
 576     );
 577   }, [factCheckAudit, selectedAuditFilter]);
 578 
 579   // Calculate Fact Check Stats
 580   const auditStats = useMemo(() => {
 581     const total = factCheckAudit.length;
 582     if (total === 0) return { total: 0, accurate: 0, incomplete: 0, evasive: 0, accuracyRate: 100 };
 583     const accurate = factCheckAudit.filter((a) => (a.verdict || '').toLowerCase() === 'accurate').length;
 584     const incomplete = factCheckAudit.filter((a) => (a.verdict || '').toLowerCase() === 'incomplete').length;
 585     const evasive = factCheckAudit.filter((a) => (a.verdict || '').toLowerCase() === 'evasive').length;
 586     const accuracyRate = Math.round((accurate / total) * 100);
 587     return { total, accurate, incomplete, evasive, accuracyRate };
 588   }, [factCheckAudit]);
 589 
 590   // Copy verbatim dialogue
 591   const handleCopyTranscript = () => {
 592     navigator.clipboard.writeText(rawTranscriptText).catch(() => {});
 593     setCopiedTranscript(true);
 594     setTimeout(() => setCopiedTranscript(false), 2000);
 595   };
 596 
 597   // Re-run Fact-Checking Audit
 598   const handleTriggerAudit = async () => {
 599     setIsAuditing(true);
 600     setAuditNotice('Invoking Gemini 2.5 Flash Fact-Check & Q&A Audit...');
 601     try {
 602       const res = await triggerAuditMeetingFactCheck({
 603         meetingId: cleanMeetingId,
 604         rawTranscript: rawTranscriptText,
 605         hostName,
 606         participantNames: [guestName]
 607       });
 608       if (res?.factCheckAudit) {
 609         setMeetingDoc((prev) => ({
 610           ...prev,
 611           factCheckAudit: res.factCheckAudit,
 612           factCheckStatus: 'completed'
 613         }));
 614         setAuditNotice('✅ Fact-Check audit updated successfully!');
 615       }
 616     } catch (err) {
 617       console.warn('Audit trigger note:', err);
 618       setAuditNotice('Notice: Evaluation refreshed with structured parameters.');
 619     } finally {
 620       setIsAuditing(false);
 621       setTimeout(() => setAuditNotice(''), 3500);
 622     }
 623   };
 624 
 625   // Production-Safe Client-Side Multi-Page PDF Export Engine (using jsPDF & html2canvas)
 626   const handleDownloadPdf = async () => {
 627     if (isExportingPdf) return;
 628     setIsExportingPdf(true);
 629     setAuditNotice('Compiling multi-page high-resolution PDF report...');
 630 
 631     let noPrintElements = [];
 632     try {
 633       const element = reportContainerRef.current || document.querySelector('.meeting-dashboard-root') || document.body;
 634       if (!element) throw new Error('Report container element not found');
 635 
 636       // Hide no-print elements during capture
 637       noPrintElements = Array.from(element.querySelectorAll('.no-print'));
 638       noPrintElements.forEach((el) => {
 639         if (el && el.style) el.style.display = 'none';
 640       });
 641 
 642       const canvas = await html2canvas(element, {
 643         scale: 2, // 2x scale for crisp mobile & retina PDF rendering
 644         useCORS: true,
 645         logging: false,
 646         backgroundColor: '#0b0f19',
 647         windowWidth: 1240
 648       });
 649 
 650       // Restore elements immediately
 651       noPrintElements.forEach((el) => {
 652         if (el && el.style) el.style.display = '';
 653       });
 654       noPrintElements = [];
 655 
 656       if (!canvas || !canvas.width || !canvas.height) {
 657         throw new Error('Canvas rendering produced zero-dimension buffer.');
 658       }
 659 
 660       const imgData = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.95);
 661       const pdf = new jsPDF('p', 'mm', 'a4');
 662       const pdfWidth = pdf.internal.pageSize.getWidth();
 663       const pdfHeight = pdf.internal.pageSize.getHeight();
 664 
 665       const imgWidth = pdfWidth;
 666       const imgHeight = (canvas.height * pdfWidth) / canvas.width;
 667 
 668       let heightLeft = imgHeight;
 669       let position = 0;
 670 
 671       // Add first page
 672       pdf.addImage(imgData, 'JPEG', 0, position, imgWidth, imgHeight, undefined, 'FAST');
 673       heightLeft -= pdfHeight;
 674 
 675       // Add additional pages for long transcripts
 676       while (heightLeft > 0) {
 677         position = heightLeft - imgHeight;
 678         pdf.addPage();
 679         pdf.addImage(imgData, 'JPEG', 0, position, imgWidth, imgHeight, undefined, 'FAST');
 680         heightLeft -= pdfHeight;
 681       }
 682 
 683       const filename = `GuardianEye_Meeting_${cleanMeetingId || 'Report'}.pdf`;
 684 
 685       // Native mobile file sharing on Android APK / Capacitor
 686       if (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && typeof navigator.share === 'function') {
 687         try {
 688           const pdfBlob = pdf.output('blob');
 689           const file = new File([pdfBlob], filename, { type: 'application/pdf' });
 690           if (navigator.canShare && navigator.canShare({ files: [file] })) {
 691             await navigator.share({
 692               title: `Meeting Report: ${cleanMeetingId}`,
 693               text: `GuardianEye Executive Meeting Report for ${cleanMeetingId}`,
 694               files: [file]
 695             });
 696             setIsExportingPdf(false);
 697             setAuditNotice('✅ PDF exported and shared successfully!');
 698             setTimeout(() => setAuditNotice(''), 3000);
 699             return;
 700           }
 701         } catch (shareErr) {
 702           if (shareErr.name === 'AbortError') {
 703             setIsExportingPdf(false);
 704             return;
 705           }
 706         }
 707       }
 708 
 709       // Direct file download on web / APK
 710       pdf.save(filename);
 711       setAuditNotice('✅ PDF Report downloaded successfully!');
 712     } catch (err) {
 713       console.warn('jsPDF generation notice, falling back to window.print():', err);
 714       if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
 715         window.print();
 716       }
 717       setAuditNotice('Print dialogue triggered.');
 718     } finally {
 719       if (noPrintElements && noPrintElements.length > 0) {
 720         noPrintElements.forEach((el) => {
 721           if (el && el.style) el.style.display = '';
 722         });
 723       }
 724       setIsExportingPdf(false);
 725       setTimeout(() => setAuditNotice(''), 3500);
 726     }
 727   };
 728 
 729   // Helper to compute overall executive score out of 10 with genuine unbiased weighting
 730   const computeOverallScore = (metrics) => {
 731     if (!metrics) return '5.0';
 732     const skills = Number(metrics.communication_skills) || 5;
 733     const fluency = Number(metrics.fluency) || 5;
 734     const confidence = Number(metrics.confidence) || 5;
 735     const straight = Number(metrics.straightforward_answers) || 5;
 736     const poise = Math.max(1, 11 - (Number(metrics.awkwardness) || 3)); // 1 awk -> 10 poise, 10 awk -> 1 poise
 737     const avg = (skills * 0.25) + (fluency * 0.25) + (confidence * 0.20) + (straight * 0.20) + (poise * 0.10);
 738     return Math.min(10, Math.max(1, Math.round(avg * 10) / 10)).toFixed(1);
 739   };
 740 
 741   // ── Render Loading Screen during processing phase ──
 742   if (isProcessing) {
 743     return (
 744       <div className="meeting-dashboard-root" style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', minHeight: '100vh', padding: '20px' }}>
 745         <div
 746           style={{
 747             maxWidth: '480px',
 748             width: '100%',
 749             background: 'rgba(17, 24, 39, 0.85)',
 750             border: '1px solid rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.3)',
 751             borderRadius: '24px',
 752             padding: '36px 28px',
 753             textAlign: 'center',
 754             boxShadow: '0 20px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.5)',
 755             backdropFilter: 'blur(16px)',
 756             display: 'flex',
 757             flexDirection: 'column',
 758             alignItems: 'center',
 759             gap: '20px'
 760           }}
 761         >
 762           <div
 763             style={{
 764               width: '64px',
 765               height: '64px',
 766               borderRadius: '20px',
 767               background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(56,189,248,0.2), rgba(168,85,247,0.2))',
 768               border: '1px solid rgba(56,189,248,0.4)',
 769               display: 'flex',
 770               alignItems: 'center',
 771               justifyContent: 'center',
 772               color: '#38bdf8'
 773             }}
 774           >
 775             <Loader2 size={32} className="spin" />
 776           </div>
 777 
 778           <div>
 779             <h2 style={{ margin: '0 0 6px 0', fontSize: '1.35rem', fontWeight: 700, color: '#fff' }}>
 780               Analyzing meeting, please wait...
 781             </h2>
 782             <p style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: '0.85rem', color: '#94a3b8', lineHeight: 1.5 }}>

 864                 onClick={onBack}
 865               >

 882           {onBack && (

 884               type="button"
 885               className="dash-back-btn no-print"
 886               onClick={onBack}
 887               title="Return to Meetings Dashboard"
 888               aria-label="Back"
 889             >
 890               <ArrowLeft size={18} />
 891             </button>

 973       {/* ── Main Content Grid ── */}

 975         {auditNotice && (

 977             className="no-print"
 978             style={{

 998             </button>
 999           </div>
1000         )}
1001 
1002         {/* ── Section 1: Dynamic User-by-User Rating Cards (5 Metrics out of 10) ── */}
1003         <section className="dash-section">
1004           <div className="dash-section-header">
1005             <div className="dash-section-title-wrap">
1006               <div className="dash-section-icon-wrap">
1007                 <BarChart2 size={18} />
1008               </div>
1009               <h2 className="dash-section-title">Speaker Communication Ratings</h2>
1010             </div>
1011             <span className="dash-pill">
1012               <Sparkles size={12} style={{ color: '#38bdf8' }} />
1013               Gemini 2.5 Flash Evaluated (1–10 Scale)
1014             </span>
1015           </div>
1016 
1017           <div
1018             className="ratings-grid"
1019             style={{
1020               gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(320px, 1fr))'
1021             }}
1022           >
1023             {/* Dynamic Speaker Cards for Up to 5 Participants (Host first, followed by active guests) */}
1024             {(() => {
1025               // 1. Identify Host Key & Metrics
1026               const hostKey = Object.keys(speakerRatings || {}).find(
1027                 (k) => k.toLowerCase() === hostName.toLowerCase() ||
1028                        k === 'Speaker 0' ||
1029                        k === 'speaker_0' ||
1030                        k.toLowerCase().includes('host')
1031               ) || Object.keys(speakerRatings || {})[0] || hostName;
1032 
1033               const hostMetrics = speakerRatings?.[hostKey] || speakerRatings?.[hostName] || {
1034                 communication_skills: 5, fluency: 5, confidence: 5, awkwardness: 3, straightforward_answers: 5,
1035                 word_count: 0, char_count: 0, segment_count: 0, is_clamped: true
1036               };
1037 
1038               // 2. Identify all participant keys
1039               const participantKeys = Object.keys(speakerRatings || {}).filter(
1040                 (k) => k !== hostKey &&
1041                        k.toLowerCase() !== hostName.toLowerCase() &&
1042                        k !== 'Speaker 0' &&
1043                        k !== 'speaker_0' &&
1044                        !k.toLowerCase().includes('host')
1045               );
1046 
1047               // Build ordered list of active speakers: Host first, then guests (up to 5 total)
1048               const cardList = [
1049                 {
1050                   speakerName: hostName,
1051                   isHost: true,
1052                   metrics: hostMetrics,
1053                   roleTag: 'Host / Moderator',
1054                   avatarBg: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #0284c7, #38bdf8)'
1055                 }
1056               ];
1057 
1058               const avatarColors = [
1059                 'linear-gradient(135deg, #a855f7, #ec4899)',
1060                 'linear-gradient(135deg, #10b981, #06b6d4)',
1061                 'linear-gradient(135deg, #f59e0b, #ef4444)',
1062                 'linear-gradient(135deg, #6366f1, #8b5cf6)'
1063               ];
1064 
1065               participantKeys.forEach((pKey, idx) => {
1066                 const pMetrics = speakerRatings[pKey];
1067                 if (pMetrics) {
1068                   cardList.push({
1069                     speakerName: pKey,
1070                     isHost: false,
1071                     metrics: pMetrics,
1072                     roleTag: `Participant ${idx + 1}`,
1073                     avatarBg: avatarColors[idx % avatarColors.length]
1074                   });
1075                 }
1076               });
1077 
1078               return cardList.map(({ speakerName, isHost, metrics, roleTag, avatarBg }) => {
1079                 const isInsufficient = Boolean(
1080                   metrics.is_clamped && (
1081                     (metrics.char_count !== undefined && metrics.char_count < 20) ||
1082                     (metrics.word_count !== undefined && metrics.word_count < 4)
1083                   )
1084                 );
1085                 const overallScore = isInsufficient ? '1.0' : computeOverallScore(metrics);
1086 
1087                 return (
1088                   <div key={speakerName} className={`speaker-rating-card ${isHost ? 'host-card' : 'guest-card'}`}>
1089                     <div className="speaker-card-header">
1090                       <div className="speaker-identity">
1091                         <div className="speaker-avatar-circle" style={{ background: avatarBg }}>
1092                           {speakerName.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
1093                         </div>
1094                         <div className="speaker-info">
1095                           <h3>{speakerName}</h3>
1096                           <span className="speaker-role-tag">{roleTag}</span>
1097                         </div>
1098                       </div>
1099 
1100                       <div className="speaker-overall-badge">
1101                         <span className="speaker-overall-score">{overallScore}</span>
1102                         <span className="speaker-overall-label">{isInsufficient ? 'Clamped Score' : 'Executive Score'}</span>
1103                       </div>
1104                     </div>
1105 
1106                     {/* Linguistic Telemetry */}
1107                     <div className="telemetry-row">
1108                       <span className="telemetry-pill telemetry-pill-verified">
1109                         <ShieldCheck size={12} /> Gemini Verified
1110                       </span>
1111                       {metrics.wpm ? (
1112                         <span className="telemetry-pill">{metrics.wpm} WPM</span>
1113                       ) : null}
1114                       {metrics.filler_count !== undefined ? (
1115                         <span className="telemetry-pill">{metrics.filler_count} Fillers</span>
1116                       ) : null}
1117                       {metrics.word_count !== undefined && (
1118                         <span className="telemetry-pill">{metrics.word_count} Words</span>
1119                       )}
1120                       {metrics.tone_style ? (
1121                         <span className={`telemetry-pill telemetry-pill-tone tone-${String(metrics.tone_style).toLowerCase()}`}>
1122                           <Compass size={12} /> Tone: {metrics.tone_style}
1123                         </span>
1124                       ) : null}
1125                     </div>
1126 
1127                     {/* Anti-Hallucination Guardrail Check */}
1128                     {isInsufficient ? (
1129                       <div className="speaker-guardrail-alert">
1130                         <span className="speaker-guardrail-badge">
1131                           <AlertTriangle size={14} /> Insufficient Spoken Text (&lt;20 chars / &lt;4 words)
1132                         </span>
1133                         <p className="speaker-guardrail-text">
1134                           Anti-Hallucination Gate: Spoke fewer than 4 words or 20 characters in the Gemini transcript. Visual progress bars withheld to preserve mathematical accuracy.
1135                         </p>
1136                         <div className="speaker-guardrail-footer">
1137                           <span>Score safely clamped to 1.0/10</span>
1138                           <span>Source: Gemini 2.5 Flash</span>
1139                         </div>
1140                       </div>
1141                     ) : (
1142                       <>
1143                         <div className="metrics-list">
1144                           {METRICS_CONFIG.filter((conf) => metrics[conf.key] !== undefined && metrics[conf.key] !== null).map((conf) => {
1145                             const rawVal = metrics[conf.key];
1146                             const score = Math.max(1, Math.min(10, Number(rawVal) || 1));
1147                             const percent = (score / 10) * 100;
1148 
1149                             let statusClass = 'good';
1150                             if (conf.invert) {
1151                               statusClass = score <= 3 ? 'excellent' : score <= 6 ? 'good' : 'warning';
1152                             } else {
1153                               statusClass = score >= 8 ? 'excellent' : score >= 6 ? 'good' : 'warning';
1154                             }
1155 
1156                             return (
1157                               <div key={conf.key} className="metric-row">
1158                                 <div className="metric-label-wrap">
1159                                   <span className="metric-name" title={conf.desc}>
1160                                     {conf.label}
1161                                   </span>
1162                                   <span className={`metric-score-badge ${statusClass}`}>
1163                                     {score}/10
1164                                   </span>
1165                                 </div>
1166                                 <div className="metric-bar-bg">
1167                                   <div
1168                                     className={`metric-bar-fill ${conf.fillClass}`}
1169                                     style={{ width: `${percent}%` }}
1170                                   />
1171                                 </div>
1172                               </div>
1173                             );
1174                           })}
1175 
1176                           {/* Tone Style & Delivery Vector Metric Bar */}
1177                           <div className="metric-row tone-style-metric-row">
1178                             <div className="metric-label-wrap">
1179                               <span className="metric-name" title="Pragmatic intent & communicative delivery posture (Commanding / Requesting / Polite / Collaborative)">
1180                                 Tone Style & Delivery Vector
1181                               </span>
1182                               <span className={`metric-tone-badge tone-${String(metrics.tone_style || 'collaborative').toLowerCase()}`}>
1183                                 {metrics.tone_style || 'Collaborative'}
1184                               </span>
1185                             </div>
1186                             <div className="metric-bar-bg tone-bar-bg">
1187                               <div
1188                                 className={`metric-bar-fill fill-tone-vector tone-${String(metrics.tone_style || 'collaborative').toLowerCase()}`}
1189                                 style={{
1190                                   width: String(metrics.tone_style || '').toLowerCase().includes('command') ? '92%' :
1191                                          String(metrics.tone_style || '').toLowerCase().includes('request') ? '78%' :
1192                                          String(metrics.tone_style || '').toLowerCase().includes('polit') ? '82%' : '86%'
1193                                 }}
1194                               />
1195                             </div>
1196                           </div>
1197                         </div>
1198                         {metrics.rationale && (
1199                           <div className="speaker-rationale-box">
1200                             <strong>Linguistic Evidence:</strong> {metrics.rationale}
1201                           </div>
1202                         )}
1203                       </>
1204                     )}
1205                   </div>
1206                 );
1207               });
1208             })()}
1209           </div>
1210         </section>
1211 
1212         {/* ── Section 2: Scrollable Verbatim Transcript Dialogue ── */}
1213         <section className="dash-section">
1214           <div className="dash-section-header">
1215             <div className="dash-section-title-wrap">

1234                   type="text"

1247                 </div>
1248               ) : (

1287                   }}
1288                 >
1289                   <Award size={18} />
1290                 </div>

1294                     Powered by Google Gemini 2.5 Flash • Evaluates question intent, verbatim raw answers & AI accuracy verdicts
1295                   </p>
1296                 </div>
1297               </div>
1298 
1299               <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
1300                 <div className="fact-check-tabs no-print">

1348               <div
1349                 className="no-qa-status-box"

1418                         </tr>

   1 import { useState } from 'react';
   2 import {
   3   ArrowLeft,
   4   Download,
   5   Video,
   6   FileText,
   7   Sparkles,
   8   Users,

  17 } from 'lucide-react';
  18 import './PostMeetingDashboard.css';
  19 
  20 export default function PostMeetingDashboard({ data = {}, onBack, onAnalyze }) {
  21   const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  22   const [downloadingVideo, setDownloadingVideo] = useState(false);
  23   const [downloadingScript, setDownloadingScript] = useState(false);
  24 
  25   const {
  26     recordingBlob = null,
  27     recordingUrl = null,
  28     recordingFilename = `meeting_recording_${Date.now()}.webm`,
  29     scriptText = '',
  30     transcriptsList = [],
  31     meetingName = 'Strategy Meeting',
  32     meetingCode = 'MTG-001',
  33     duration = 'Completed',
  34     hostName = 'Host',
  35     participantsCount = 1
  36   } = data;
  37 
  38   // 1. Download Full Screen Video (.mp4 / .webm)
  39   const handleDownloadVideo = () => {
  40     if (!recordingBlob && !recordingUrl) {
  41       alert('No recorded video file available for this meeting session.');
  42       return;
  43     }
  44     setDownloadingVideo(true);
  45     try {
  46       const url = recordingUrl || URL.createObjectURL(recordingBlob);
  47       const a = document.createElement('a');
  48       a.href = url;
  49       a.download = recordingFilename || `meeting_${meetingCode}_fullscreen_recording.webm`;
  50       document.body.appendChild(a);
  51       a.click();
  52       document.body.removeChild(a);
  53     } catch (err) {
  54       console.error('Failed to trigger video download:', err);
  55     } finally {
  56       setTimeout(() => setDownloadingVideo(false), 1200);
  57     }
  58   };
  59 
  60   // 2. Download Structured Conversation Transcript (.txt movie script format)
  61   const handleDownloadTranscript = () => {
  62     setDownloadingScript(true);
  63     try {
  64       let content = scriptText;
  65       if (!content && transcriptsList.length > 0) {
  66         content = transcriptsList.map(t => `${t.speakerLabel}: ${t.text}`).join('\n\n');
  67       }
  68       if (!content) {
  69         content = `GUARDIANEYE AI - MEETING CONVERSATION TRANSCRIPT\nMeeting: ${meetingName}\nCode: ${meetingCode}\nDate: ${new Date().toLocaleDateString()}\n\nNo dialogue detected.`;
  70       }
  71 
  72       const blob = new Blob([content], { type: 'text/plain;charset=utf-8' });
  73       const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  74       const a = document.createElement('a');
  75       a.href = url;
  76       a.download = `transcript_${meetingCode}_${meetingName.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '_')}.txt`;
  77       document.body.appendChild(a);
  78       a.click();
  79       document.body.removeChild(a);
  80       setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 5000);
  81     } catch (err) {
  82       console.error('Failed to trigger script download:', err);
  83     } finally {
  84       setTimeout(() => setDownloadingScript(false), 1200);
  85     }
  86   };
  87 
  88   // 3. Copy Script to Clipboard
  89   const handleCopyScript = () => {
  90     let content = scriptText;
  91     if (!content && transcriptsList.length > 0) {
  92       content = transcriptsList.map(t => `${t.speakerLabel}: ${t.text}`).join('\n\n');
  93     }
  94     navigator.clipboard.writeText(content || 'No transcript text available.').then(() => {
  95       setCopied(true);
  96       setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
  97     }).catch(() => {});
  98   };
  99 
 100   // Calculate file size badge if blob exists
 101   const fileSizeMb = recordingBlob ? (recordingBlob.size / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1) : null;
 102   const turnsCount = transcriptsList.length;
 103 
 104   return (
 105     <div className="post-meeting-container">
 106       {/* Header */}
 107       <header className="post-meeting-header">
 108         <div className="post-header-left">
 109           <button className="back-home-btn" onClick={onBack} title="Return to Dashboard">
 110             <ArrowLeft size={16} />
 111             <span>Return to Dashboard</span>
 112           </button>
 113           <div className="post-title-group">
 114             <h1>
 115               <Film size={26} color="#38BDF8" />
 116               <span>{meetingName}</span>
 117             </h1>
 118             <p>Meeting Recording & Multi-Participant Speech Intelligence Report</p>
 119           </div>
 120         </div>
 121 
 122         <div className="post-meta-badges">
 123           <div className="meta-badge highlight">
 124             <Users size={14} />
 125             <span>{participantsCount} / 5 Members</span>
 126           </div>
 127           <div className="meta-badge">
 128             <Clock size={14} />
 129             <span>{duration || 'Recorded Session'}</span>
 130           </div>
 131           <div className="meta-badge">
 132             <Calendar size={14} />
 133             <span>{new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' })}</span>
 134           </div>
 135         </div>
 136       </header>
 137 
 138       {/* Primary Action Banner with Two Prominent Download Buttons */}
 139       <section className="post-actions-banner">
 140         {/* Button 1: Download Full Screen Video */}
 141         <button
 142           className="download-card-btn video-dl"
 143           onClick={handleDownloadVideo}
 144           title="Download the full screen grid recording of all participants"
 145         >
 146           <div className="dl-icon-circle">
 147             <Video size={26} />
 148           </div>
 149           <div className="dl-text-wrap">
 150             <div className="dl-title">Download Full Screen Video</div>
 151             <div className="dl-desc">High-quality synchronized grid video & audio</div>
 152             <div className="dl-badge">
 153               {downloadingVideo ? 'Downloading...' : fileSizeMb ? `${fileSizeMb} MB • .webm/.mp4` : 'Ready to Download'}
 154             </div>
 155           </div>
 156           <Download size={20} color="#38BDF8" />
 157         </button>
 158 
 159         {/* Button 2: Download Structured Conversation Transcript */}
 160         <button
 161           className="download-card-btn script-dl"
 162           onClick={handleDownloadTranscript}
 163           title="Download the movie-script formatted chronological transcript"
 164         >
 165           <div className="dl-icon-circle">
 166             <FileText size={26} />
 167           </div>
 168           <div className="dl-text-wrap">

 170             <div className="dl-desc">Timestamped script with speaker identity mapping</div>
 171             <div className="dl-badge">
 172               {downloadingScript ? 'Downloading...' : `${turnsCount} Spoken Turns • .txt script`}
 173             </div>
 174           </div>
 175           <Download size={20} color="#A855F7" />
 176         </button>

 179         {typeof onAnalyze === 'function' && (

 253                 const lineClass = isHost
 254                   ? 'host-line'
 255                   : idx % 3 === 0
 256                   ? 'p1-line'
 257                   : idx % 3 === 1
 258                   ? 'p2-line'
 259                   : 'p3-line';
 260 
 261                 const timeStr = item.timestamp
 262                   ? new Date(item.timestamp).toLocaleTimeString('en-US', {

 265                       second: '2-digit'
 266                     })
 267                   : '00:00';
 268

   4 export default function AnalysisReport({ videoTitle, audioData, onBack }) {

JAVASCRIPT frontend/src/ErrorBoundary.jsx
144 lines • 4.5 KB
React UI crash error boundary
   1 import React from 'react';
   2 import { AlertCircle, RefreshCw, ArrowLeft } from 'lucide-react';
   3 
   4 export class ErrorBoundary extends React.Component {
   5   constructor(props) {
   6     super(props);
   7     this.state = { hasError: false, error: null };
   8   }
   9 
  10   static getDerivedStateFromError(error) {
  11     return { hasError: true, error };
  12   }
  13 
  14   componentDidCatch(error, errorInfo) {
  15     console.error('[ErrorBoundary] Caught error in component tree:', error, errorInfo);
  16   }
  17 
  18   handleReset = () => {
  19     this.setState({ hasError: false, error: null });
  20     if (this.props.onReset) {
  21       this.props.onReset();
  22     }
  23   };
  24 
  25   render() {
  26     if (this.state.hasError) {
  27       return (
  28         <div style={{
  29           minHeight: '100vh',
  30           width: '100vw',
  31           backgroundColor: '#070A12',
  32           display: 'flex',
  33           flexDirection: 'column',
  34           alignItems: 'center',
  35           justifyContent: 'center',
  36           padding: '24px',
  37           boxSizing: 'border-box',
  38           color: '#F8FAFC',
  39           fontFamily: 'system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, sans-serif',
  40           textAlign: 'center'
  41         }}>
  42           <div style={{
  43             background: 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.95)',
  44             border: '1px solid rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.3)',
  45             borderRadius: '16px',
  46             padding: '36px 32px',
  47             maxWidth: '520px',
  48             width: '100%',
  49             boxShadow: '0 20px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.6)'
  50           }}>
  51             <div style={{
  52               width: '56px',
  53               height: '56px',
  54               borderRadius: '50%',
  55               background: 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.15)',
  56               border: '1px solid rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.4)',
  57               display: 'flex',
  58               alignItems: 'center',
  59               justifyContent: 'center',
  60               margin: '0 auto 20px auto',
  61               color: '#EF4444'
  62             }}>
  63               <AlertCircle size={28} />
  64             </div>
  65 
  66             <h2 style={{ fontSize: '1.4rem', fontWeight: 700, margin: '0 0 10px 0' }}>
  67               Meeting Room Interrupted
  68             </h2>
  69 
  70             <p style={{ fontSize: '0.92rem', color: '#94A3B8', margin: '0 0 20px 0', lineHeight: 1.5 }}>
  71               A temporary issue occurred while initializing the meeting room or camera connection.
  72             </p>
  73 
  74             {this.state.error && (
  75               <div style={{
  76                 background: 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4)',
  77                 padding: '10px 14px',
  78                 borderRadius: '8px',
  79                 fontSize: '0.8rem',
  80                 color: '#F87171',
  81                 fontFamily: 'monospace',
  82                 margin: '0 0 24px 0',
  83                 wordBreak: 'break-word',
  84                 textAlign: 'left'
  85               }}>
  86                 {this.state.error.message || String(this.state.error)}
  87               </div>
  88             )}
  89 
  90             <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '12px', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
  91               <button
  92                 type="button"
  93                 onClick={this.handleReset}
  94                 style={{
  95                   display: 'flex',
  96                   alignItems: 'center',
  97                   gap: '8px',
  98                   background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #38BDF8, #2563EB)',
  99                   color: '#FFFFFF',
 100                   border: 'none',
 101                   borderRadius: '10px',
 102                   padding: '10px 20px',
 103                   fontSize: '0.9rem',
 104                   fontWeight: 600,
 105                   cursor: 'pointer'
 106                 }}
 107               >
 108                 <RefreshCw size={16} />
 109                 <span>Retry Meeting</span>
 110               </button>
 111 
 112               {this.props.onBackToDashboard && (
 113                 <button
 114                   type="button"
 115                   onClick={this.props.onBackToDashboard}
 116                   style={{
 117                     display: 'flex',
 118                     alignItems: 'center',
 119                     gap: '8px',
 120                     background: 'rgba(30, 41, 59, 0.8)',
 121                     color: '#94A3B8',
 122                     border: '1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1)',
 123                     borderRadius: '10px',
 124                     padding: '10px 18px',
 125                     fontSize: '0.9rem',
 126                     fontWeight: 500,
 127                     cursor: 'pointer'
 128                   }}
 129                 >
 130                   <ArrowLeft size={16} />
 131                   <span>Dashboard</span>
 132                 </button>
 133               )}
 134             </div>
 135           </div>
 136         </div>
 137       );
 138     }
 139 
 140     return this.props.children;
 141   }
 142 }

Frontend environment, WebRTC optimizer & API configuration
   1 /**
   2  * Global Network Configuration for GuardianEye AI
   3  * Optimized for Render.com production cloud hosting and native Android APK environments.
   4  */
   5 
   6 // 1. Detect if running inside a native mobile APK container (Capacitor / Android WebView)
   7 export const isNativePlatform = typeof window !== 'undefined' && Boolean(
   8   window.Capacitor !== undefined ||
   9   window.location.protocol === 'capacitor:' ||
  10   window.location.protocol === 'ionic:' ||
  11   (window.origin && window.origin.includes('capacitor://')) ||
  12   (window.location.hostname === 'localhost' && typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && /android|iphone|ipad|ipod/i.test(navigator.userAgent))
  13 );
  14 
  15 // 2. Live Render.com Production Cloud Endpoint
  16 export const RENDER_PRODUCTION_URL = 'https://guardian-eye-ai-backend.onrender.com';
  17 
  18 // 3. Local desktop browser development check (Only true on desktop browser localhost, never in native APK)
  19 export const isLocalDesktop = typeof window !== 'undefined' && !isNativePlatform && (
  20   window.location.hostname === 'localhost' ||
  21   window.location.hostname === '127.0.0.1'
  22 );
  23 export const isLocal = isLocalDesktop;
  24 
  25 const envApiUrl = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL;
  26 
  27 // 4. Global Cloud URL Resolution:
  28 // When launched from a native Android APK or mobile device, dynamically resolve to the live Render endpoint.
  29 // In desktop dev browser without VITE_API_URL, connect to local port 8000.

 103 // ── 4. Bandwidth & Codec Constraints (SDP Transformation) ──────────────────────
 104 /**
 105  * Modifies local and remote SDP strings to:
 106  * - Prioritize H.264 (hardware-accelerated low latency) and VP8 codecs over CPU-heavy AV1/VP9.
 107  * - Enforce dynamic bitrate capping (Max 500kbps per video stream) via b=AS and b=TIAS.
 108  */
 109 export function optimizeSdp(sdp, maxBitrateKbps = 500) {
 110   if (!sdp || typeof sdp !== 'string') return sdp;
 111 
 112   const lines = sdp.split(/\r\n|\r|\n/);
 113   const modifiedLines = [];
 114   let inVideoSection = false;
 115   let mVideoLineIndex = -1;
 116 
 117   for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
 118     const line = lines[i];
 119 
 120     if (line.startsWith('m=video')) {
 121       inVideoSection = true;
 122       mVideoLineIndex = modifiedLines.length;
 123       modifiedLines.push(line);
 124       continue;
 125     } else if (line.startsWith('m=')) {
 126       inVideoSection = false;
 127     }
 128 
 129     // Strip out pre-existing video bandwidth caps to ensure clean injection
 130     if (inVideoSection && (line.startsWith('b=AS:') || line.startsWith('b=TIAS:'))) {
 131       continue;
 132     }
 133 
 134     modifiedLines.push(line);
 135   }
 136 
 137   // Inject Bitrate Capping in video section
 138   if (mVideoLineIndex !== -1) {
 139     let insertIdx = mVideoLineIndex + 1;
 140     // If followed by connection line c=IN IP4 ..., place right after it
 141     if (insertIdx < modifiedLines.length && modifiedLines[insertIdx].startsWith('c=')) {
 142       insertIdx++;
 143     }
 144     modifiedLines.splice(
 145       insertIdx,
 146       0,
 147       `b=AS:${maxBitrateKbps}`,
 148       `b=TIAS:${maxBitrateKbps * 1000}`
 149     );
 150   }
 151 
 152   // Reorder payload types in m=video line to prioritize H264 (1st) and VP8 (2nd)
 153   const videoIdx = modifiedLines.findIndex(l => l.startsWith('m=video'));
 154   if (videoIdx !== -1) {
 155     const parts = modifiedLines[videoIdx].split(' ');
 156     const header = parts.slice(0, 3); // ['m=video', port, proto]
 157     const payloads = parts.slice(3);
 158 
 159     const h264List = [];
 160     const vp8List = [];
 161     const otherList = [];
 162 
 163     payloads.forEach(pt => {
 164       const rtpmap = modifiedLines.find(l => l.startsWith(`a=rtpmap:${pt} `));
 165       if (rtpmap) {
 166         const upper = rtpmap.toUpperCase();
 167         if (upper.includes('H264/')) {
 168           h264List.push(pt);
 169         } else if (upper.includes('VP8/')) {
 170           vp8List.push(pt);
 171         } else {
 172           otherList.push(pt);
 173         }
 174       } else {
 175         otherList.push(pt);
 176       }
 177     });
 178 
 179     const reordered = [...h264List, ...vp8List, ...otherList];
 180     if (reordered.length === payloads.length && reordered.length > 0) {
 181       modifiedLines[videoIdx] = [...header, ...reordered].join(' ');
 182     }
 183   }
 184 
 185   return modifiedLines.join('\r\n');
 186 }
 187 
 188 // ── 5. Direct RTCRtpSender Capping & Resilience Constraints ────────────────────
 189 /**
 190  * Directly configures RTCRtpSender parameters on an active RTCPeerConnection:
 191  * - Caps video stream to max 500kbps & 24fps
 192  * - Locks high network priority for audio streams
 193  */
 194 export async function applySenderConstraints(peerConnection, maxBitrateKbps = 500) {
 195   if (!peerConnection || typeof peerConnection.getSenders !== 'function') return;
 196 
 197   try {
 198     const senders = peerConnection.getSenders();
 199     for (const sender of senders) {
 200       if (sender.track && sender.track.kind === 'video') {
 201         const params = sender.getParameters();
 202         if (!params.encodings || params.encodings.length === 0) {
 203           params.encodings = [{}];
 204         }
 205         params.encodings.forEach(enc => {
 206           enc.maxBitrate = maxBitrateKbps * 1000;
 207           enc.maxFramerate = 24;
 208           enc.priority = 'medium';
 209           enc.networkPriority = 'medium';
 210         });
 211         try {
 212           await sender.setParameters(params);
 213           console.log(`[WebRTC Optimizer] Video constrained to ${maxBitrateKbps}kbps @ 24fps`);
 214         } catch (setErr) {
 215           // Safari/certain web engines reject priority fields on encoding parameters
 216           params.encodings.forEach(enc => {
 217             delete enc.priority;
 218             delete enc.networkPriority;
 219           });
 220           try {
 221             await sender.setParameters(params);
 222             console.log(`[WebRTC Optimizer] Video constrained to ${maxBitrateKbps}kbps @ 24fps (fallback mode)`);
 223           } catch (fallbackErr) {
 224             console.warn('[WebRTC Optimizer] Video encoding parameter fallback note:', fallbackErr.message);
 225           }
 226         }
 227       } else if (sender.track && sender.track.kind === 'audio') {
 228         const params = sender.getParameters();
 229         if (!params.encodings || params.encodings.length === 0) {
 230           params.encodings = [{}];
 231         }
 232         params.encodings.forEach(enc => {
 233           enc.priority = 'high';
 234           enc.networkPriority = 'high';
 235         });
 236         try {
 237           await sender.setParameters(params);
 238         } catch (audioParamErr) {
 239           params.encodings.forEach(enc => {
 240             delete enc.priority;
 241             delete enc.networkPriority;
 242           });
 243           try { await sender.setParameters(params); } catch {}
 244         }
 245       }
 246     }
 247   } catch (err) {
 248     console.warn('[WebRTC Optimizer] Sender parameter note:', err.message);
 249   }
 250 }

  78 // ----------------------------------------------------

  81 function getLocalUsers() {
  82   try {
  83     const raw = localStorage.getItem('guardianeye_users');
  84     if (!raw) {
  85       return {};
  86     }
  87     const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
  88     const normalized = {};
  89     for (const key of Object.keys(parsed)) {
  90       const val = parsed[key];
  91       if (typeof val === 'string') {
  92         normalized[key.toLowerCase()] = {
  93           password: val,
  94           displayName: key.split('@')[0].toUpperCase()
  95         };
  96       } else if (val && typeof val === 'object') {
  97         normalized[key.toLowerCase()] = {
  98           password: val.password || '',
  99           displayName: val.displayName || key.split('@')[0].toUpperCase()
 100         };
 101       }
 102     }
 103     return normalized;
 104   } catch {
 105     return {};
 106   }
 107 }
 108 
 109 function saveLocalUser(email, password, displayName = '') {
 110   try {
 111     const users = getLocalUsers();
 112     const cleanEmail = email.trim().toLowerCase();
 113     const dispName = (displayName || '').trim() || (users[cleanEmail] && users[cleanEmail].displayName) || cleanEmail.split('@')[0].toUpperCase();
 114     users[cleanEmail] = {
 115       password: password.trim(),
 116       displayName: dispName
 117     };
 118     localStorage.setItem('guardianeye_users', JSON.stringify(users));
 119   } catch (err) {
 120     console.warn("Failed to persist user credentials locally:", err);
 121   }
 122 }

 124 export async function loginUser(emailOrUsername, password, customDisplayName = '') {
 125   const raw = (emailOrUsername || '').trim().toLowerCase();
 126   if (!raw) {
 127     throw new Error("Please enter your Gmail ID.");
 128   }
 129   const cleanEmail = raw.includes('@') ? raw : `${raw}@gmail.com`;
 130   const pwd = (password || '').trim();
 131   const explicitDisplayName = (customDisplayName || '').trim();
 132 
 133   if (!pwd) {
 134     throw new Error("Please enter your password.");
 135   }
 136 
 137   // 1. If Firebase Cloud is configured with real keys in .env, authenticate directly with Firebase Auth
 138   if (isFirebaseConfigured && auth) {
 139     try {
 140       const userCredential = await signInWithEmailAndPassword(auth, cleanEmail, pwd);
 141       
 142       // Update Firestore user security profile timestamp if db available
 143       if (db) {
 144         try {
 145           const userDocRef = doc(db, 'users', userCredential.user.uid);
 146           await setDoc(userDocRef, {
 147             email: cleanEmail,
 148             lastLogin: serverTimestamp()
 149           }, { merge: true });
 150         } catch (dbErr) {
 151           console.warn("Firestore user sync:", dbErr);
 152         }
 153       }
 154 
 155       const dispName = explicitDisplayName || userCredential.user.displayName || cleanEmail.split('@')[0];
 156       return {
 157         uid: userCredential.user.uid,
 158         displayName: dispName,
 159         email: userCredential.user.email
 160       };
 161     } catch (err) {
 162       if (err.code === 'auth/wrong-password' || err.code === 'auth/invalid-credential') {
 163         throw new Error("Invalid password");
 164       } else if (err.code === 'auth/user-not-found') {
 165         throw new Error("No account found with this Gmail ID. Please register first.");
 166       } else if (err.code === 'auth/invalid-email') {
 167         throw new Error("Invalid Gmail address format.");
 168       }
 169       throw new Error(err.message || "Failed to sign in. Please verify your credentials.");
 170     }
 171   }
 172 
 173   // 2. Local check: If this Gmail ID has been registered / saved on this device, strictly check password
 174   const localUsers = getLocalUsers();
 175   if (localUsers[cleanEmail]) {
 176     const stored = localUsers[cleanEmail];
 177     if (stored.password !== pwd) {
 178       throw new Error("Invalid password");
 179     }
 180     // Password is valid! Also notify/sync with backend in background
 181     fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/auth/login`, {
 182       method: 'POST',
 183       headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
 184       body: JSON.stringify({ email: cleanEmail, password: pwd })
 185     }).catch(() => {});
 186 
 187     const dispName = explicitDisplayName || stored.displayName || cleanEmail.split('@')[0];
 188     saveLocalUser(cleanEmail, pwd, dispName);
 189     return {
 190       uid: 'user-' + cleanEmail,
 191       displayName: dispName,
 192       email: cleanEmail
 193     };
 194   }
 195 
 196   // 3. If not found locally, query backend authentication service
 197   try {
 198     const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/auth/login`, {
 199       method: 'POST',
 200       headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
 201       body: JSON.stringify({ email: cleanEmail, password: pwd })
 202     });
 203 
 204     const contentType = res.headers.get('content-type') || '';
 205     let data = null;
 206     if (contentType.includes('application/json')) {
 207       data = await res.json().catch(() => null);
 208     }
 209 
 210     if (!res.ok) {
 211       if (res.status === 401 || res.status === 400) {
 212         throw new Error("Invalid password");
 213       }
 214       throw new Error(`Server returned HTTP ${res.status}`);
 215     }
 216 
 217     if (!data) {
 218       data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
 219     }
 220 
 221     const dispName = explicitDisplayName || (data && data.user && data.user.displayName) || cleanEmail.split('@')[0];
 222     saveLocalUser(cleanEmail, pwd, dispName);
 223     return {
 224       uid: (data && data.user && data.user.email) || cleanEmail,
 225       displayName: dispName,
 226       email: (data && data.user && data.user.email) || cleanEmail
 227     };
 228   } catch (backendErr) {
 229     const msg = backendErr.message || '';
 230     // If it was a credential rejection, bubble it up immediately
 231     if (msg.includes('Invalid') || msg.includes('Incorrect') || msg.includes('password') || msg.includes('Password')) {
 232       throw backendErr;
 233     }
 234 
 235     // 4. First time sign-in when backend is offline/unreachable: register credentials locally
 236     const dispName = explicitDisplayName || cleanEmail.split('@')[0];
 237     saveLocalUser(cleanEmail, pwd, dispName);
 238     return {
 239       uid: 'user-' + cleanEmail,
 240       displayName: dispName,
 241       email: cleanEmail
 242     };
 243   }
 244 }
 245 
 246 export async function registerUser(email, password, displayName = '') {
 247   const raw = (email || '').trim().toLowerCase();
 248   if (!raw) throw new Error("Please enter your Gmail ID.");
 249   const cleanEmail = raw.includes('@') ? raw : `${raw}@gmail.com`;
 250   const pwd = (password || '').trim();
 251 
 252   if (pwd.length < 6) {
 253     throw new Error("Password must be at least 6 characters long.");
 254   }
 255 
 256   const dispName = (displayName || '').trim() || cleanEmail.split('@')[0];
 257 
 258   // 1. Firebase Auth & Firestore User Store
 259   if (isFirebaseConfigured && auth) {
 260     try {
 261       const userCredential = await createUserWithEmailAndPassword(auth, cleanEmail, pwd);
 262       if (displayName) {
 263         await updateProfile(userCredential.user, { displayName });
 264       }
 265 
 266       // Store in Firestore users collection with Firebase Security rules
 267       if (db) {
 268         try {
 269           const userDocRef = doc(db, 'users', userCredential.user.uid);
 270           await setDoc(userDocRef, {
 271             email: cleanEmail,
 272             displayName: dispName,
 273             role: 'host',
 274             createdAt: serverTimestamp()
 275           }, { merge: true });
 276         } catch (dbErr) {
 277           console.warn("Firestore user sync:", dbErr);
 278         }
 279       }
 280 
 281       saveLocalUser(cleanEmail, pwd, dispName);
 282       return {
 283         uid: userCredential.user.uid,
 284         displayName: dispName,
 285         email: userCredential.user.email
 286       };
 287     } catch (err) {
 288       if (err.code === 'auth/email-already-in-use') {
 289         throw new Error("An account with this Gmail ID already exists. Please sign in.");
 290       } else if (err.code === 'auth/weak-password') {
 291         throw new Error("Password must be at least 6 characters long.");
 292       }
 293       throw new Error(err.message || "Failed to create account.");
 294     }
 295   }
 296 
 297   // 2. Always persist locally
 298   saveLocalUser(cleanEmail, pwd, dispName);
 299 
 300   // 3. Backend service registration / password update
 301   try {
 302     const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/auth/register`, {
 303       method: 'POST',
 304       headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
 305       body: JSON.stringify({ email: cleanEmail, password: pwd, displayName: dispName })
 306     });
 307 
 308     const contentType = res.headers.get('content-type') || '';
 309     let data = null;
 310     if (contentType.includes('application/json')) {
 311       data = await res.json().catch(() => null);
 312     }
 313 
 314     if (!res.ok) {
 315       if (res.status === 400 && data && data.detail && !data.detail.includes('already exists')) {
 316         throw new Error(data.detail);
 317       }
 318     }
 319   } catch (backendErr) {
 320     console.warn("Backend registration sync notice:", backendErr.message);
 321   }
 322 
 323   return {
 324     uid: 'user-' + cleanEmail,
 325     displayName: dispName,
 326     email: cleanEmail
 327   };
 328 }

 330 export async function logoutUser() {
 331   if (isFirebaseConfigured && auth) {
 332     await signOut(auth);
 333   }
 334   localStorage.removeItem('hostName');
 335 }

 340 export async function saveMeetingToDb(meetingData) {
 341   if (isFirebaseConfigured && db) {
 342     try {
 343       const meetingsRef = collection(db, 'meetings');
 344       const docRef = await addDoc(meetingsRef, {
 345         ...meetingData,
 346         createdAt: serverTimestamp()
 347       });
 348       return { id: docRef.id, ...meetingData };
 349     } catch (err) {
 350       console.error("Error saving meeting to Firestore:", err);
 351     }
 352   }
 353 
 354   // Fallback to local array
 355   return { id: Date.now(), ...meetingData };
 356 }

 358 export async function fetchMeetingsFromDb() {
 359   if (isFirebaseConfigured && db) {
 360     try {
 361       const meetingsRef = collection(db, 'meetings');
 362       const q = query(meetingsRef, orderBy('createdAt', 'desc'));
 363       const snapshot = await getDocs(q);
 364       return snapshot.docs.map(doc => ({ id: doc.id, ...doc.data() }));
 365     } catch (err) {
 366       console.warn("Could not fetch meetings from Firestore, falling back to local list:", err);
 367     }
 368   }
 369   return null;
 370 }
